ju̱ṣethā̍ṁ ya̱jñam bodha̍ta̱ṁ hava̍sya me̱ viśve̱ha de̍vau̱ sava̱nāva̍ gacchatam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ ceṣa̍ṁ no voḻham aśvinā ||

**8.35.5**

stoma̍ṁ juṣethāṁ yuva̱śeva̍ ka̱nyanā̱ṁ viśve̱ha de̍vau̱ sava̱nāva̍ gacchatam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ ceṣa̍ṁ no voḻham aśvinā ||

**8.35.6**

giro̍ juṣethām adhva̱raṁ ju̍ṣethā̱ṁ viśve̱ha de̍vau̱ sava̱nāva̍ gacchatam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ ceṣa̍ṁ no voḻham aśvinā ||

**8.35.7**

hā̱ri̱dra̱veva̍ patatho̱ vaned upa̱ soma̍ṁ su̱tam ma̍hi̱ṣevāva̍ gacchathaḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ trir va̱rtir yā̍tam aśvinā ||

**8.35.8**

ha̱ṁsāv i̍va patatho adhva̱gāv i̍va̱ soma̍ṁ su̱tam ma̍hi̱ṣevāva̍ gacchathaḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ trir va̱rtir yā̍tam aśvinā ||

**8.35.9**

śye̱nāv i̍va patatho ha̱vyadā̍taye̱ soma̍ṁ su̱tam ma̍hi̱ṣevāva̍ gacchathaḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ trir va̱rtir yā̍tam aśvinā ||

**8.35.10**

piba̍taṁ ca tṛpṇu̱taṁ cā ca̍ gacchatam pra̱jāṁ ca̍ dha̱ttaṁ dravi̍ṇaṁ ca dhattam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ corja̍ṁ no dhattam aśvinā ||

**8.35.11**

jaya̍taṁ ca̱ pra stu̍taṁ ca̱ pra cā̍vatam pra̱jāṁ ca̍ dha̱ttaṁ dravi̍ṇaṁ ca dhattam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ corja̍ṁ no dhattam aśvinā ||

**8.35.12**

ha̱taṁ ca̱ śatrū̱n yata̍taṁ ca mi̱triṇa̍ḥ pra̱jāṁ ca̍ dha̱ttaṁ dravi̍ṇaṁ ca dhattam | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ corja̍ṁ no dhattam aśvinā ||

**8.35.13**

mi̱trāvaru̍ṇavantā u̱ta dharma̍vantā ma̱rutva̍ntā jari̱tur ga̍cchatho̱ hava̍m | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa cādi̱tyair yā̍tam aśvinā ||

**8.35.14**

aṅgi̍rasvantā u̱ta viṣṇu̍vantā ma̱rutva̍ntā jari̱tur ga̍cchatho̱ hava̍m | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa cādi̱tyair yā̍tam aśvinā ||

**8.35.15**

ṛ̱bhu̱mantā̍ vṛṣaṇā̱ vāja̍vantā ma̱rutva̍ntā jari̱tur ga̍cchatho̱ hava̍m | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa cādi̱tyair yā̍tam aśvinā ||

**8.35.16**

brahma̍ jinvatam u̱ta ji̍nvata̱ṁ dhiyo̍ ha̱taṁ rakṣā̍ṁsi̱ sedha̍ta̱m amī̍vāḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍ṁ sunva̱to a̍śvinā ||

**8.35.17**

kṣa̱traṁ ji̍nvatam u̱ta ji̍nvata̱ṁ nṝn ha̱taṁ rakṣā̍ṁsi̱ sedha̍ta̱m amī̍vāḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍ṁ sunva̱to a̍śvinā ||

**8.35.18**

dhe̱nūr ji̍nvatam u̱ta ji̍nvata̱ṁ viśo̍ ha̱taṁ rakṣā̍ṁsi̱ sedha̍ta̱m amī̍vāḥ | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa ca̱ soma̍ṁ sunva̱to a̍śvinā ||

**8.35.19**

atre̍r iva śṛṇutam pū̱rvyastu̍tiṁ śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to ma̍dacyutā | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ cāśvi̍nā ti̱roa̍hnyam ||

**8.35.20**

sargā̍m̐ iva sṛjataṁ suṣṭu̱tīr upa̍ śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to ma̍dacyutā | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ cāśvi̍nā ti̱roa̍hnyam ||

**8.35.21**

ra̱śmīm̐r i̍va yacchatam adhva̱rām̐ upa̍ śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to ma̍dacyutā | sa̱joṣa̍sā u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ cāśvi̍nā ti̱roa̍hnyam ||

**8.35.22**

a̱rvāg ratha̱ṁ ni ya̍cchata̱m piba̍taṁ so̱myam madhu̍ | ā yā̍tam aśvi̱nā ga̍tam ava̱syur vā̍m a̱haṁ hu̍ve dha̱ttaṁ ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**8.35.23**

na̱mo̱vā̱ke prasthi̍te adhva̱re na̍rā vi̱vakṣa̍ṇasya pī̱taye̍ | ā yā̍tam aśvi̱nā ga̍tam ava̱syur vā̍m a̱haṁ hu̍ve dha̱ttaṁ ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**8.35.24**

svāhā̍kṛtasya tṛmpataṁ su̱tasya̍ devā̱v andha̍saḥ | ā yā̍tam aśvi̱nā ga̍tam ava̱syur vā̍m a̱haṁ hu̍ve dha̱ttaṁ ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**8.36.1**

a̱vi̱tāsi̍ sunva̱to vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣa̱ḥ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||

**8.36.2**

prāva̍ sto̱tāra̍m maghava̱nn ava̱ tvām pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||

**8.36.3**

ū̱rjā de̱vām̐ ava̱sy oja̍sā̱ tvām pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||

**8.36.4**

ja̱ni̱tā di̱vo ja̍ni̱tā pṛ̍thi̱vyāḥ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||

**8.36.5**

ja̱ni̱tāśvā̍nāṁ jani̱tā gavā̍m asi̱ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||

**8.36.6**

atrī̍ṇā̱ṁ stoma̍m adrivo ma̱has kṛ̍dhi̱ pibā̱ soma̱m madā̍ya̱ kaṁ śa̍takrato | yaṁ te̍ bhā̱gam adhā̍raya̱n viśvā̍ḥ sehā̱naḥ pṛta̍nā u̱ru jraya̱ḥ sam a̍psu̱jin ma̱rutvā̍m̐ indra satpate ||

**8.36.7**

śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱tas tathā̍ śṛṇu̱ yathāśṛ̍ṇo̱r atre̱ḥ karmā̍ṇi kṛṇva̱taḥ | pra tra̱sada̍syum āvitha̱ tvam eka̱ in nṛ̱ṣāhya̱ indra̱ brahmā̍ṇi va̱rdhaya̍n ||

**8.37.1**

predam brahma̍ vṛtra̱tūrye̍ṣv āvitha̱ pra su̍nva̱taḥ śa̍cīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||

**8.37.2**

se̱hā̱na u̍gra̱ pṛta̍nā a̱bhi druha̍ḥ śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||

**8.37.3**

e̱ka̱rāḻ a̱sya bhuva̍nasya rājasi śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||

**8.37.4**

sa̱sthāvā̍nā yavayasi̱ tvam eka̱ ic cha̍cīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||

**8.37.5**

kṣema̍sya ca pra̱yuja̍ś ca̱ tvam ī̍śiṣe śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||

**8.37.6**

kṣa̱trāya̍ tva̱m ava̍si̱ na tva̍m āvitha śacīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | mādhya̍ṁdinasya̱ sava̍nasya vṛtrahann anedya̱ pibā̱ soma̍sya vajrivaḥ ||

**8.37.7**

śyā̱vāśva̍sya̱ rebha̍ta̱s tathā̍ śṛṇu̱ yathāśṛ̍ṇo̱r atre̱ḥ karmā̍ṇi kṛṇva̱taḥ | pra tra̱sada̍syum āvitha̱ tvam eka̱ in nṛ̱ṣāhya̱ indra̍ kṣa̱trāṇi̍ va̱rdhaya̍n ||

**8.38.1**

ya̱jñasya̱ hi stha ṛ̱tvijā̱ sasnī̱ vāje̍ṣu̱ karma̍su | indrā̍gnī̱ tasya̍ bodhatam ||

**8.38.2**

to̱śāsā̍ ratha̱yāvā̍nā vṛtra̱haṇāpa̍rājitā | indrā̍gnī̱ tasya̍ bodhatam ||

**8.38.3**

i̱daṁ vā̍m madi̱ram madhv adhu̍kṣa̱nn adri̍bhi̱r nara̍ḥ | indrā̍gnī̱ tasya̍ bodhatam ||

**8.38.4**

ju̱ṣethā̍ṁ ya̱jñam i̱ṣṭaye̍ su̱taṁ soma̍ṁ sadhastutī | indrā̍gnī̱ ā ga̍taṁ narā ||

**8.38.5**

i̱mā ju̍ṣethā̱ṁ sava̍nā̱ yebhi̍r ha̱vyāny ū̱hathu̍ḥ | indrā̍gnī̱ ā ga̍taṁ narā ||

**8.38.6**

i̱māṁ gā̍ya̱trava̍rtaniṁ ju̱ṣethā̍ṁ suṣṭu̱tim mama̍ | indrā̍gnī̱ ā ga̍taṁ narā ||

**8.38.7**

prā̱ta̱ryāva̍bhi̱r ā ga̍taṁ de̱vebhi̍r jenyāvasū | indrā̍gnī̱ soma̍pītaye ||

**8.38.8**

śyā̱vāśva̍sya sunva̱to 'trī̍ṇāṁ śṛṇuta̱ṁ hava̍m | indrā̍gnī̱ soma̍pītaye ||

**8.38.9**

e̱vā vā̍m ahva ū̱taye̱ yathāhu̍vanta̱ medhi̍rāḥ | indrā̍gnī̱ soma̍pītaye ||

**8.38.10**

āhaṁ sara̍svatīvator indrā̱gnyor avo̍ vṛṇe | yābhyā̍ṁ gāya̱tram ṛ̱cyate̍ ||

**8.39.1**

a̱gnim a̍stoṣy ṛ̱gmiya̍m a̱gnim ī̱ḻā ya̱jadhyai̍ | a̱gnir de̱vām̐ a̍naktu na u̱bhe hi vi̱dathe̍ ka̱vir a̱ntaś cara̍ti dū̱tya1̱̍ṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.2**

ny a̍gne̱ navya̍sā̱ vaca̍s ta̱nūṣu̱ śaṁsa̍m eṣām | ny arā̍tī̱ rarā̍vṇā̱ṁ viśvā̍ a̱ryo arā̍tīr i̱to yu̍cchantv ā̱muro̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.3**

agne̱ manmā̍ni̱ tubhya̱ṁ kaṁ ghṛ̱taṁ na ju̍hva ā̱sani̍ | sa de̱veṣu̱ pra ci̍kiddhi̱ tvaṁ hy asi̍ pū̱rvyaḥ śi̱vo dū̱to vi̱vasva̍to̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.4**

tat-ta̍d a̱gnir vayo̍ dadhe̱ yathā̍-yathā kṛpa̱ṇyati̍ | ū̱rjāhu̍ti̱r vasū̍nā̱ṁ śaṁ ca̱ yoś ca̱ mayo̍ dadhe̱ viśva̍syai de̱vahū̍tyai̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.5**

sa ci̍keta̱ sahī̍yasā̱gniś ci̱treṇa̱ karma̍ṇā | sa hotā̱ śaśva̍tīnā̱ṁ dakṣi̍ṇābhir a̱bhīvṛ̍ta i̱noti̍ ca pratī̱vya1̱̍ṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.6**

a̱gnir jā̱tā de̱vānā̍m a̱gnir ve̍da̱ martā̍nām apī̱cya̍m | a̱gniḥ sa dra̍viṇo̱dā a̱gnir dvārā̱ vy ū̍rṇute̱ svā̍huto̱ navī̍yasā̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.7**

a̱gnir de̱veṣu̱ saṁva̍su̱ḥ sa vi̱kṣu ya̱jñiyā̱sv ā | sa mu̱dā kāvyā̍ pu̱ru viśva̱m bhūme̍va puṣyati de̱vo de̱veṣu̍ ya̱jñiyo̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.8**

yo a̱gniḥ sa̱ptamā̍nuṣaḥ śri̱to viśve̍ṣu̱ sindhu̍ṣu | tam āga̍nma tripa̱styam ma̍ndhā̱tur da̍syu̱hanta̍mam a̱gniṁ ya̱jñeṣu̍ pū̱rvyaṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.9**

a̱gnis trīṇi̍ tri̱dhātū̱ny ā kṣe̍ti vi̱dathā̍ ka̱viḥ | sa trīm̐r e̍kāda̱śām̐ i̱ha yakṣa̍c ca pi̱praya̍c ca no̱ vipro̍ dū̱taḥ pari̍ṣkṛto̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.39.10**

tvaṁ no̍ agna ā̱yuṣu̱ tvaṁ de̱veṣu̍ pūrvya̱ vasva̱ eka̍ irajyasi | tvām āpa̍ḥ pari̱sruta̱ḥ pari̍ yanti̱ svase̍tavo̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.1**

indrā̍gnī yu̱vaṁ su na̱ḥ saha̍ntā̱ dāsa̍tho ra̱yim | yena̍ dṛ̱ḻhā sa̱matsv ā vī̱ḻu ci̍t sāhiṣī̱mahy a̱gnir vane̍va̱ vāta̱ in nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.2**

na̱hi vā̍ṁ va̱vrayā̍ma̱he 'thendra̱m id ya̍jāmahe̱ śavi̍ṣṭhaṁ nṛ̱ṇāṁ nara̍m | sa na̍ḥ ka̱dā ci̱d arva̍tā̱ gama̱d ā vāja̍sātaye̱ gama̱d ā me̱dhasā̍taye̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.3**

tā hi madhya̱m bharā̍ṇām indrā̱gnī a̍dhikṣi̱taḥ | tā u̍ kavitva̱nā ka̱vī pṛ̱cchyamā̍nā sakhīya̱te saṁ dhī̱tam a̍śnutaṁ narā̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.4**

a̱bhy a̍rca nabhāka̱vad i̍ndrā̱gnī ya̱jasā̍ gi̱rā | yayo̱r viśva̍m i̱daṁ jaga̍d i̱yaṁ dyauḥ pṛ̍thi̱vī ma̱hy u1̱̍pasthe̍ bibhṛ̱to vasu̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.5**

pra brahmā̍ṇi nabhāka̱vad i̍ndrā̱gnibhyā̍m irajyata | yā sa̱pta bu̍dhnam arṇa̱vaṁ ji̱hmabā̍ram aporṇu̱ta indra̱ īśā̍na̱ oja̍sā̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.6**

api̍ vṛśca purāṇa̱vad vra̱tate̍r iva guṣpi̱tam ojo̍ dā̱sasya̍ dambhaya | va̱yaṁ tad a̍sya̱ sambhṛ̍ta̱ṁ vasv indre̍ṇa̱ vi bha̍jemahi̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.7**

yad i̍ndrā̱gnī janā̍ i̱me vi̱hvaya̍nte̱ tanā̍ gi̱rā | a̱smāke̍bhi̱r nṛbhi̍r va̱yaṁ sā̍sa̱hyāma̍ pṛtanya̱to va̍nu̱yāma̍ vanuṣya̱to nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.8**

yā nu śve̱tāv a̱vo di̱va u̱ccarā̍ta̱ upa̱ dyubhi̍ḥ | i̱ndrā̱gnyor anu̍ vra̱tam uhā̍nā yanti̱ sindha̍vo̱ yān sī̍m ba̱ndhād amu̍ñcatā̱ṁ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.9**

pū̱rvīṣ ṭa̍ i̱ndropa̍mātayaḥ pū̱rvīr u̱ta praśa̍staya̱ḥ sūno̍ hi̱nvasya̍ harivaḥ | vasvo̍ vī̱rasyā̱pṛco̱ yā nu sādha̍nta no̱ dhiyo̱ nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.10**

taṁ śi̍śītā suvṛ̱ktibhi̍s tve̱ṣaṁ satvā̍nam ṛ̱gmiya̍m | u̱to nu ci̱d ya oja̍sā̱ śuṣṇa̍syā̱ṇḍāni̱ bheda̍ti̱ jeṣa̱t sva̍rvatīr a̱po nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.11**

taṁ śi̍śītā svadhva̱raṁ sa̱tyaṁ satvā̍nam ṛ̱tviya̍m | u̱to nu ci̱d ya oha̍ta ā̱ṇḍā śuṣṇa̍sya̱ bheda̱ty ajai̍ḥ sva̍rvatīr a̱po nabha̍ntām anya̱ke sa̍me ||

**8.40.12**

e̱vendrā̱gnibhyā̍m pitṛ̱van navī̍yo mandhātṛ̱vad a̍ṅgira̱svad a̍vāci | tri̱dhātu̍nā̱ śarma̍ṇā pātam a̱smān va̱yaṁ syā̍ma̱ pata̍yo rayī̱ṇām ||

**8.41.1**

asmā ū ṣu prabhūtaye varuṇāya marudbhyo 'rcā viduṣṭarebhyaḥ | yo dhītā mānuṣāṇām paśvo gā iva rakṣati nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.2**

tam ū ṣu samanā girā pitṝṇāṁ ca manmabhiḥ | nābhākasya praśastibhir yaḥ sindhūnām upodaye saptasvasā sa madhyamo nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.3**

sa kṣapaḥ pari ṣasvaje ny usro māyayā dadhe sa viśvam pari darśataḥ | tasya venīr anu vratam uṣas tisro avardhayan nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.4**

yaḥ kakubho nidhārayaḥ pṛthivyām adhi darśataḥ | sa mātā pūrvyam padaṁ tad varuṇasya saptyaṁ sa hi gopā iveryo nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.5**

yo dhartā bhuvanānāṁ ya usrāṇām apīcyā3 veda nāmāni guhyā | sa kaviḥ kāvyā puru rūpaṁ dyaur iva puṣyati nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.6**

yasmin viśvāni kāvyā cakre nābhir iva śritā | tritaṁ jūtī saparyata vraje gāvo na saṁyuje yuje aśvām̐ ayukṣata nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.7**

ya āsv atka āśaye viśvā jātāny eṣām | pari dhāmāni marmṛśad varuṇasya puro gaye viśve devā anu vrataṁ nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.8**

sa samudro apīcyas turo dyām iva rohati ni yad āsu yajur dadhe | sa māyā arcinā padāstṛṇān nākam āruhan nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.9**

yasya śvetā vicakṣaṇā tisro bhūmīr adhikṣitaḥ | trir uttarāṇi papratur varuṇasya dhruvaṁ sadaḥ sa saptānām irajyati nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.41.10**

yaḥ śvetām̐ adhinirṇijaś cakre kṛṣṇām̐ anu vratā | sa dhāma pūrvyam mame yaḥ skambhena vi rodasī ajo na dyām adhārayan nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.42.1**

astabhnād dyām asuro viśvavedā amimīta varimāṇam pṛthivyāḥ | āsīdad viśvā bhuvanāni samrāḍ viśvet tāni varuṇasya vratāni ||

**8.42.2**

evā vandasva varuṇam bṛhantaṁ namasyā dhīram amṛtasya gopām | sa naḥ śarma trivarūthaṁ vi yaṁsat pātaṁ no dyāvāpṛthivī upasthe ||

**8.42.3**

imāṁ dhiyaṁ śikṣamāṇasya deva kratuṁ dakṣaṁ varuṇa saṁ śiśādhi | yayāti viśvā duritā tarema sutarmāṇam adhi nāvaṁ ruhema ||

**8.42.4**

ā vāṁ grāvāṇo aśvinā dhībhir viprā acucyavuḥ | nāsatyā somapītaye nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.42.5**

yathā vām atrir aśvinā gīrbhir vipro ajohavīt | nāsatyā somapītaye nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.42.6**

evā vām ahva ūtaye yathāhuvanta medhirāḥ | nāsatyā somapītaye nabhantām anyake same ||

**8.43.1**

ime viprasya vedhaso 'gner astṛtayajvanaḥ | giraḥ stomāsa īrate ||

**8.43.2**

asmai te pratiharyate jātavedo vicarṣaṇe | agne janāmi suṣṭutim ||

**8.43.3**

ārokā iva ghed aha tigmā agne tava tviṣaḥ | dadbhir vanāni bapsati ||

**8.43.4**

harayo dhūmaketavo vātajūtā upa dyavi | yatante vṛthag agnayaḥ ||

**8.43.5**

ete tye vṛthag agnaya iddhāsaḥ sam adṛkṣata | uṣasām iva ketavaḥ ||

**8.43.6**

kṛṣṇā rajāṁsi patsutaḥ prayāṇe jātavedasaḥ | agnir yad rodhati kṣami ||

**8.43.7**

dhāsiṁ kṛṇvāna oṣadhīr bapsad agnir na vāyati | punar yan taruṇīr api ||

**8.43.8**

jihvābhir aha nannamad arciṣā jañjaṇābhavan | agnir vaneṣu rocate ||

**8.43.9**

apsv agne sadhiṣ ṭava sauṣadhīr anu rudhyase | garbhe saṁ jāyase punaḥ ||

**8.43.10**

ud agne tava tad ghṛtād arcī rocata āhutam | niṁsānaṁ juhvo3 mukhe ||

**8.43.11**

ukṣānnāya vaśānnāya somapṛṣṭhāya vedhase | stomair vidhemāgnaye ||

**8.43.12**

uta tvā namasā vayaṁ hotar vareṇyakrato | agne samidbhir īmahe ||

**8.43.13**

uta tvā bhṛguvac chuce manuṣvad agna āhuta | aṅgirasvad dhavāmahe ||

**8.43.14**

tvaṁ hy agne agninā vipro vipreṇa san satā | sakhā sakhyā samidhyase ||

**8.43.15**

sa tvaṁ viprāya dāśuṣe rayiṁ dehi sahasriṇam | agne vīravatīm iṣam ||

**8.43.16**

agne bhrātaḥ sahaskṛta rohidaśva śucivrata | imaṁ stomaṁ juṣasva me ||

**8.43.17**

uta tvāgne mama stuto vāśrāya pratiharyate | goṣṭhaṁ gāva ivāśata ||

**8.43.18**

tubhyaṁ tā aṅgirastama viśvāḥ sukṣitayaḥ pṛthak | agne kāmāya yemire ||

**8.43.19**

agniṁ dhībhir manīṣiṇo medhirāso vipaścitaḥ | admasadyāya hinvire ||

**8.43.20**

taṁ tvām ajmeṣu vājinaṁ tanvānā agne adhvaram | vahniṁ hotāram īḻate ||

**8.43.21**

purutrā hi sadṛṅṅ asi viśo viśvā anu prabhuḥ | samatsu tvā havāmahe ||

**8.43.22**

tam īḻiṣva ya āhuto 'gnir vibhrājate ghṛtaiḥ | imaṁ naḥ śṛṇavad dhavam ||

**8.43.23**

taṁ tvā vayaṁ havāmahe śṛṇvantaṁ jātavedasam | agne ghnantam apa dviṣaḥ ||

**8.43.24**

viśāṁ rājānam adbhutam adhyakṣaṁ dharmaṇām imam | agnim īḻe sa u śravat ||

**8.43.25**

agniṁ viśvāyuvepasam maryaṁ na vājinaṁ hitam | saptiṁ na vājayāmasi ||

**8.43.26**

ghnan mṛdhrāṇy apa dviṣo dahan rakṣāṁsi viśvahā | agne tigmena dīdihi ||

**8.43.27**

yaṁ tvā janāsa indhathe manuṣvad aṅgirastama | agne sa bodhi me vacaḥ ||

**8.43.28**

yad agne divijā asy apsu jā vā sahaskṛta | taṁ tvā gīrbhir havāmahe ||

**8.43.29**

tubhyaṁ ghet te janā ime viśvāḥ sukṣitayaḥ pṛthak | dhāsiṁ hinvanty attave ||

**8.43.30**

te ghed agne svādhyo 'hā viśvā nṛcakṣasaḥ | tarantaḥ syāma durgahā ||

**8.43.31**

agnim mandram purupriyaṁ śīram pāvakaśociṣam | hṛdbhir mandrebhir īmahe ||

**8.43.32**

sa tvam agne vibhāvasuḥ sṛjan sūryo na raśmibhiḥ | śardhan tamāṁsi jighnase ||

**8.43.33**

tat te sahasva īmahe dātraṁ yan nopadadyati | tvad agne vāryaṁ vasu ||

**8.44.1**

samidhāgniṁ duvasyata ghṛtair bodhayatāti thim | āsmin havyā juhotana ||

**8.44.2**

agne stomaṁ juṣasva me vardhasvānena manmanā | prati sūktāni harya naḥ ||

**8.44.3**

agniṁ dūtam puro dadhe havyavāham upa bruve | devām̐ ā sādayād iha ||

**8.44.4**

ut te bṛhanto arcayaḥ samidhānasya dīdivaḥ | agne śukrāsa īrate ||

**8.44.5**

upa tvā juhvo3 mama ghṛtācīr yantu haryata | agne havyā juṣasva naḥ ||

**8.44.6**

mandraṁ hotāram ṛtvijam citrabhānum vibhāvasum | agnim īḻe sa u śravat ||

**8.44.7**

pratnaṁ hotāram īḍyaṁ juṣṭam agniṁ kavikratum | adhvarāṇām abhiśriyam ||

**8.44.8**

juṣāṇo aṅgirastamemā havyāny ānuṣak | agne yajñaṁ naya ṛtuthā ||

**8.44.9**

samidhāna u santya śukraśoca ihā vaha | cikitvān daivyaṁ janam ||

**8.44.10**

vipraṁ hotāram adruhaṁ dhūmaketum vibhāvasum | yajñānāṁ ketum īmahe ||

**8.44.11**

agne ni pāhi nas tvam prati ṣma deva rīṣataḥ | bhindhi dveṣaḥ sahaskṛta ||

**8.44.12**

agniḥ pratnena manmanā śumbhānas tanvaṁ svām | kavir vipreṇa vāvṛdhe ||

**8.44.13**

ūrjo napātam ā huve 'gnim pāvakaśociṣam | asmin yajñe svadhvare ||

**8.44.14**

sa no mitramaha s tvam agne śukreṇa śociṣā | devair ā satsi barhiṣi ||

**8.44.15**

yo agniṁ tanvo3 dame devam martaḥ saparyati | tasmā id dīdayad vasu ||

**8.44.16**

agnir mūrdhā divaḥ kakut patiḥ pṛthivyā ayam | apāṁ retāṁsi jinvati ||

**8.44.17**

ud agne śucayas tava śukrā bhrājanta īrate ||  tava jyotīṁṣy arcayaḥ ||

**8.44.18**

īśiṣe vāryasya hi dātrasya agne svarpatiḥ | stotā syāṁ tava śarmaṇi ||

**8.44.19**

tvām agne manīṣiṇas tvāṁ hinvanti cittibhiḥ | tvāṁ vardhantu no giraḥ ||

**8.44.20**

adadbhasya svadhāvato dūtasya rebhataḥ sadā | agneḥ sakhyaṁ vṛṇīmahe ||

**8.44.21**

agniḥ śucivratatamaḥ śucir vipraḥ śuciḥ kaviḥ | śucī rocata āhutaḥ ||

**8.44.22**

uta tvā dhītayo mama giro vardhantu viśvahā | agne sakhyasya bodhi naḥ ||

**8.44.23**

yad agne syām ahaṁ tvaṁ tvaṁ vā ghā syā aham | syuṣ ṭe satyā ihāśiṣaḥ ||

**8.44.24**

vasur vasupatiḥ hi kam asy agne vibhāvasuḥ | syāma te sumatāv api ||

**8.44.25**

agne dhṛtavratāya te samudrāyeva sindhuvaḥ | giro vāśrāsa īrate ||

**8.44.26**

yuvānaṁ viśpatiṁ kaviṁ viśvādam puruvepasam | agniṁ śumbhāmi manmabhiḥ ||

**8.44.27**

yajñānāṁ rathye vayaṁ tigmajambhāya vīḻave | stomair iṣemāgnaye ||

**8.44.28**

ayam agne tve api jaritā bhūtu santya | tasmai pāvaka mṛḻaya ||

**8.44.29**

dhīro hy asy admasad vipro na jāgṛviḥ sadā | agne dīdayasi dyavi ||

**8.44.30**

purāgne duritebhyaḥ purā mṛdhrebhyaḥ kave | pra ṇa āyur vaso tira ||

**8.45.1**

ā ghā ye agnim indhate stṛṇanti barhir ānuṣak | yeṣām indro yuvā sakhā ||

**8.45.2**

bṛhann id idhma eṣām bhūri śastam pṛthuḥ svaru | yeṣām indro yuvā sakhā ||

**8.45.3**

ayuddha id yudhā vṛtaṁ śūra ājaṭi satvabhiḥ | yeṣām indro yuvā sakhā ||

**8.45.4**

ā bundaṁ vṛtrahā dade jātaḥ pṛcchad vi mātaram | ka ugrāḥ ke ha śṛṇvire ||

**8.45.5**

prati tvā śavasī vadan girāv apso na yodhiṣat | yas te śatrutvaṁ ācake ||

**8.45.6**

uta tvam maghavañ chṛṇu yas te vaṣṭi vavakṣi tat | yad vīḻayāsi vīḻu tat ||

**8.45.7**

yad ājiṁ yāty ājikṛd indraḥ svaśvayur upa | rathītamo rathīnām ||

**8.45.8**

vi ṣu viśvā abhiyujo vajrin viṣvag yathā vṛha | bhavā naḥ suśravastama ||

**8.45.9**

asmākaṁ su ratham pura indraḥ kṛṇotu sātaye | na yaṁ dhūrvanti dhūrtayaḥ ||

**8.45.10**

vṛjyāma te pari dviṣo 'raṁ te śakra dāvane | gamemedindra gomataḥ ||

**8.45.11**

śanaiś cid yanto adrivo 'śvāvantaḥ śatagvinaḥ | vivakṣaṇā anehasa ||

**8.45.12**

ūrdhvā hi te dive-dive sahasrā sūnṛtā śatā | jaritṛbhyo vimaṁhate ||

**8.45.13**

vidmā hi tvā dhanañjayam indra dṛḻhā cid ārujaṁ | ādāriṇaṁ yathā gayam ||

**8.45.14**

kakuhaṁ cit tvā kave mandantu dhṛṣṇav indavaḥ | ā tvā paṇiṁ yad īmahe ||

**8.45.15**

yas te revām̐ adāśuriḥ pramamarṣa maghattaye | tasya no veda ā bhara ||

**8.45.16**

ima u tvā vi cakṣate sakhāya indra somin aḥ | puṣṭāvanto yathā paśum ||

**8.45.17**

uta tvābadhhiraṁ vayaṁ śrutkarṇaṁ santam ūtaye | dūrād iha havāmahe ||

**8.45.18**

yac chu śrūyā imaṁ havam durmarṣaṁ cakriyā uta | bhaver āpir no antamaḥ ||

**8.45.19**

yac cid dhi te api vyathir jagaṇvāṁso amamahi | godā id indra bodhi naḥ ||

**8.45.20**

ā tvā rambhaṁ na jivriyo rarabhmā śavasas pate | uśmasi tvā sadhastha ā ||

**8.45.21**

stotram indrāya gāyata purunṛmṇāya satvane | nakir yaṁ vṛṇvate yudhi ||

**8.45.22**

abhi tvā vṛṣabhā sute sutaṁ sṛjāmi pītaye | tṛmpā vy aśnuhī madam ||

**8.45.23**

mā tvā mūrā aviṣyavo mopa hasvanā ā dabhan | mākīṁ brahmadviṣo vanaḥ ||

**8.45.24**

iha tvā goparīṇasā mahe mandantu rādhase | saro gauro yathā piba ||

**8.45.25**

yā vṛtrahā parāvati sanā navā ca cucyuve | tā saṁsatsu pra vocata ||

**8.45.26**

apibat kadruvam sutam indraḥ sahasrabāhve | atrādediṣṭa pauṁsyam ||

**8.45.27**

satyaṁ tat turvaśe yadau vidāno ahnavāyyam | vy ānaṭ turvaṇe śami ||

**8.45.28**

taraṇiṁ vo janānāṁ tradaṁ vājasya gomataḥ | samānam u pra śaṁsiṣam ||

**8.45.29**

ṛbhukṣaṇaṁ na vartava uktheṣu tugryāvṛdham | indraṁ some sacā sute ||

**8.45.30**

yaḥ kṛntad id vi yonyaṁ triśokāya girim pṛthum | gobhyo gātuṁ niretave ||

**8.45.31**

yad dadhiṣe manasyasi mandānaḥ pred iyakṣasi | mā tat kar indra mṛḻaya ||

**8.45.32**

dabhrañ cid dhi tvāvataḥ kṛtaṁ śṛṇve adhi kṣami | jigātv indra te manaḥ ||

**8.45.33**

taved u tāḥ sukīrtayo 'sann uta praśastayaḥ | yad indra mṛḻayāsi naḥ ||

**8.45.34**

mā na ekasmin āgasi mā dvayor uta triṣu | vadhīr mā śūra bhūriṣu ||

**8.45.35**

bibhayā hi tvāvata ugrād abhiprabhaṅgiṇaḥ | dasmād aham ṛtīṣahaḥ ||

**8.45.36**

mā sakhyuḥ śūnam ā vide mā putrasya prabhūvaso | āvṛtvad bhūtu te manaḥ ||

**8.45.37**

ko nu maryā amithitaḥ sakhā sakhāyam abravīt | jahā ko asmad īṣate ||

**8.45.38**

evāre vṛṣabhā sute 'sinvan bhūry āvayaḥ | śvaghnīva nivatā caran ||

**8.45.39**

ā ta etā vacoyujā harī gṛbhṇe sumadrathā | yad īm brahmabhya id dadaḥ ||

**8.45.40**

bhindhi viśvā apa dviṣaḥ pari bādho jahī mṛdhaḥ | vasu spārhaṁ tad ā bhara ||

**8.45.41**

yad vīḻāv indra yat sthire yat parśāne parābhṛtam | vasu spārhaṁ tad ā bhara ||

**8.45.42**

yasya te viśvamānuṣo bhūrer dattasya vedati | vasu spārhaṁ tad ā bhara ||

**8.46.1**

tvāvataḥ purūvaso vayam indra praṇetaḥ | smasi sthātar harīṇām ||

**8.46.2**

tvāṁ hi satyam adrivo vidma dātāram iṣām | vidma dātāraṁ rayīṇām ||

**8.46.3**

ā yasya te mahimānaṁ śatamūte śatakrato | gīrbhir gṛṇanti kāravāḥ ||

**8.46.4**

sunītho ghā sa martyo yaṁ maruto yam aryamā | mitraḥ pānty adruhaḥ ||

**8.46.5**

dadhāno gomad aśvavat suvīryam ādityajūta edhate | sadā rāyā purusprihā ||

**8.46.6**

tam indraṁ dānam īmahe śavasānam abhīrvam | īśānaṁ rāya īmahe ||

**8.46.7**

tasmin hi santy ūtayo viśvā abhīravaḥ sacā | tam ā vahantu saptayaḥ purūvasaṁ madāya harayas sutam ||

**8.46.8**

yas te mado vareṇyo ya indra vṛtrahantamaḥ | ya ādadiḥ svar nṛbhir yaḥ pṛtanāsu duṣṭaraḥ ||

**8.46.9**

yo duṣṭaro viśvavāra śrāvāyyo vājeṣu asti tarutā | sa naḥ śaviṣṭha savanā vaso gahi gamema gomati vraje ||

**8.46.10**

gavyo ṣu ṇo yathā purāśvayota rathayā | varivasya mahāmaha ||

**8.46.11**

nahi te śūra rādhaso 'ntaṁ vindāmi satrā | daśasyā no maghavan nū cid adrivo dhiyo vājebhir āvitha ||

**8.46.12**

ya ṛṣvaḥ śrāvayatsa khā viśvet sa veda janimā puruṣṭutaḥ | taṁ viśve mānuṣā yugendraṁ havante taviṣaṁ yatasrucaḥ ||

**8.46.13**

sa no vājeṣu avitā purūvusuḥ puraḥsthātā maghavā vṛtrahā bhuvan ||

**8.46.14**

abhi vo vīram andhaso made ṣu gāya girā mahā vīcetasam | indraṁ nāma śrutyaṁ śākinaṁ vaco yathā ||

**8.46.15**

dadī rekṇas tanve dadir vasu dadir vājeṣu puruhūta vājinam | nūnam atha ||

**8.46.16**

viśveṣām irajyantaṁ vasūnāṁ sāsahvāṁsaṁ cid asya varpasaḥ | kṛpayato nūnam aty atha ||

**8.46.17**

mahaḥ su vo aram iṣe stavāmahe mīḻhuṣe araṁgamāya jagmaye | yajñebhir gīrbhir viśvamanṣām marutām iyakṣasi gāye tvā namasā girā ||

**8.46.18**

ye pātayante ajmabhir girīṇāṁ snubhir eṣām | yajñam mahiṣvaṇīnāṁ sumnaṁ tuviṣvaṇīnāṁ prādhvare ||

**8.46.19**

prabhaṅgaṁ durmatīnām indra śaviṣṭhā bhara | rayim asmabhyaṁ yujyaṁ codayanmate jyeṣṭhaṁ codayanmate ||

**8.46.20**

sanitaḥ susanitar ugra citra cetiṣṭha sūnṛta | prāsahā samrāṭ sahuriṁ sahantam bhujyuṁ vājeṣu pūrvyam ||

**8.46.21**

ā sa etu ya īvad āṁ adevaḥ pūrtam ādade | yathā cid vaśo aśvyaḥ pṛthuśravasi kānīte3 'syā vyuṣy ādade ||

**8.46.22**

ṣaṣṭiṁ sahasrāśvyasyāyutāsanam uṣṭrānāṁ viṁśatiṁ śatā | daśa śyāvīnāṁ śatā daśa tryaruṣīṇāṁ daśa gavāṁ sahasrā ||

**8.46.23**

daśa śyāvā ṛdhadra yo vītavārāsa āśavaḥ | mathrā nemiṁ ni vāvṛtuḥ ||

**8.46.24**

dānāsaḥ pṛthuśravasaḥ kānītasya surādhasaḥ | rathaṁ hiraṇyayaṁ dadan maṁhiṣṭhaḥ sūrir abhūd varṣiṣṭham akṛta śravaḥ ||

**8.46.25**

ā no vāyo mahe tane yāhi makhāya pājase | vayaṁ hi te cakṛmā bhūri dāvane sadyaś cin mahi dāvane ||

**8.46.26**

yo aśvebhir vahate vasta usrās triḥ sapta saptatīnām | ebhiḥ somebhiḥ somasudbhiḥ somapā dānāya śukrapūtapāḥ ||

**8.46.27**

yo ma imaṁ cid u tmanāmandac citraṁ dāvane | araṭve akṣe nahuṣe sukṛtvani sukṛttarāya sukratuḥ ||

**8.46.28**

ucathye3 vapuṣi yaḥ svarāḷ uta vāyo ghṛtasnāḥ | aśveṣitaṁ rajeṣitaṁ śuneṣitam prājma tad idaṁ nu tat ||

**8.46.29**

adha priyam iṣirāya ṣaṣṭiṁ sahasrāsanam | aśvānān na vṛṣṇām ||

**8.46.30**

gāvo na yūtham upa yanti vadhrayā upa mā yanti vadhrayaḥ ||

**8.46.31**

adha yac cārathe gaṇe śatam uṣṭrām̐ acigradat | adha śvitneṣu viṁśatiṁ śatā ||

**8.46.32**

śataṁ dāse balbūthe vipras tarukṣa ā dade | te te vāyav ime janā madantīndragopā madanti devagopāḥ ||

**8.46.33**

adha syā yoṣaṇā mahī pratīcī vaśam aśvyam | adhirukmā vi nīyate ||

**8.47.1**

mahi vo mahatām avo varuṇa mitra dāśuṣe | yam ādityā abhi druho rakṣathā nem aghaṁ naśad anehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.2**

vidā devā aghānām ādityāso apākṛtim | pakṣā vayo yathopari vy asme śarma yacchatānehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.3**

vy asme adhi śarma tat pakṣā vayo na yantana | viśvāni viśvavedaso varūthyā manāmahe 'nehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.4**

yasmā arāsata kṣayaṁ jīvātuṁ ca pracetasaḥ | manor viśvasya ghed ima ādityā rāya īśate 'nehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.5**

pari ṇo vṛṇajann aghā durgāṇi rathyo yathā | syāmed indrasya śarmaṇy ādityānām utāvasy anehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.6**

parihvṛted anā jano yuṣmādattasya vāyati | devā adabhram āśa vo yam ādityā ahetanāne haso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.7**

na taṁ tigmaṁ cana tyajo na drāsad abhi taṁ guru | yasmā u śarma sapratha ādityāso arādhvam anehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.8**

yuṣme devā api ṣmasi yudhyanta iva varmasū | yūyam maho na enaṣo yūyam arbhād uruṣyatānehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.9**

aditir na uruṣyatv aditiḥ śarma yacchatu | mātā mitrasya revato 'ryamṇo varuṇasya cānehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.10**

yad devāḥ śarma śaraṇaṁ yad bhadraṁ yad anāturam | tridhātu yad varūthyaṁ tad asmāsu vi yantanānehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.11**

ādityā ava hi khyatādhi kūlād iva spaśaḥ | sutīrtham arvato yathānu no neṣathā sugam anehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.12**

neha bhadraṁ rakṣasvine nāvayai nopayā uta | gave ca bhadraṁ dhenave vīrāya ca śrasyate 'nehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.13**

yad āvir yad apīcyaṁ devāso asti duṣkṛtam | trite tad viśvam āptya āre asmad dadhātanānehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.14**

yac ca goṣu duṣṣvapnyaṁ yac cāsme duhitar divaḥ | tritāya tad vibhāvary āptyāya parā vahānehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.15**

niṣkaṁ vā ghā kṛṇavate srajaṁ vā duhitar divaḥ | trite duṣṣvapnyaṁ sarvam āptye pari dadmasy anehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.16**

tad annāya tad apase tam bhāgam upaseduṣe | tritāya ca dvitāya coṣo duṣṣvapnyaṁ vahānehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.17**

yathā kalāṁ yathā śaphaṁ yatha ṛṇaṁ saṁnayāmasi | evā duṣṣvapnyaṁ sarvam āptye saṁ nayāmasy anehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.47.18**

ajaiṣmādyāsanāma cābhūmānāgaso vayam | uṣo yasmād duṣṣvapnyād abhaiṣmāpa tad ucchatv anehaso va ūtayaḥ suūtayo va ūtayaḥ ||

**8.48.1**

svādor abhakṣi vayasaḥ sumedhāḥ svādhyo varivovitta rasya | viśve yaṁ devā uta martyāso madhu bruvanto abhi saṁcaranti ||

**8.48.2**

antaś ca prāgā aditir bhavāsy avayātā haraṣo daivyasya | inda v indrasya sakhyaṁ juṣāṇaḥ śrauṣṭīva dhuram anu rāya ṛdhyāḥ ||

**8.48.3**

apāma somam amṛtā abhūmāganma jyotir avidāma devān | kiṁ nūnam asmān kṛṇavad arātiḥ kim u dhūrtir amṛta martyasya ||

**8.48.4**

śaṁ no bhava hṛda ā pīta indo piteva soma sūnave suśevaḥ | sakheva sakhya uruśaṁsa dhīraḥ pra ṇa āyur jīvase soma tārīḥ ||

**8.48.5**

ime mā pītā yaśasas uruṣyavo rathaṁ na gāvaḥ sam anāha parvasū | te mā rakṣantu visrasaś caritrād uta mā srāmād yavayantv indavaḥ ||

**8.48.6**

agniṁ na mā mathitaṁ saṁ didīpaḥ pra cakṣaya kṛṇuhi vasya so naḥ | athā hi te mada ā soma manye revām̐ iva pra carā puṣṭim accha ||

**8.48.7**

iṣireṇa te manasā sutasya bhakṣīmahi pitryas yeva rāyaḥ | soma rājan pra ṇa āyūṁṣi tārī r ahānīva sūryo vāsarāṇi ||

**8.48.8**

soma rājan mṛḻayā naḥ svasti tava smasi vratyās tasya viddhi | alarti dakṣa uta manyur indo mā no aryo anukāmam parā dāḥ ||

**8.48.9**

tvaṁ hi nas tanvaḥ soma gopā gātre-gātre niṣasatthā nṛcakṣāḥ | yat te vayam pra mināma vratāni sa no mṛḻa suṣakhā deva vasya ||

**8.48.10**

ṛdūdareṇa sakhyā saceya yo mā na riṣyed dharyaśva pītaḥ | ayaṁ yaḥ somo ny adhāyy asme tasmā indram pratira m emy āyuḥ ||

**8.48.11**

apa tyā asthur anirā amīvā nir atrasan tamiṣīcīr abhai ṣuḥ | ā somo asmām̐ aruha d vihāyā aganma yatra pratirant a āyuḥ ||

**8.48.12**

yo na indu ḥ pitaro hṛtsu pīto 'martyo martyām̐ āviveśa | tasmai somāya haviṣā vidhema mṛḻīke asya sumatau syāma ||

**8.48.13**

tvaṁ soma pitṛbhiḥ saṁvidāno 'nu dyāvāpṛthivī ā tatantha | tasmai ta indo haviṣā vidhema vayaṁ syāma patayo rayīṇām ||

**8.48.14**

trātāro devā adhi vocatā no mā no nidrā īśata mota jalpiḥ | vayaṁ somasya viśvaha priyāsaḥ suvīrāso vidatham ā vadema ||

**8.48.15**

tvaṁ naḥ soma viśvato vayodhās tvaṁ svarvid ā viśā nṛcakṣāḥ | tvaṁ na inda ūtibhiḥ sajoṣāḥ pāhi paścātād uta vā purastāt ||

**8.49.1**

abhi pra vaḥ surādhasam indram arca yathā vide | yo jaritṛbhyo maghavā purūvasuḥ sahasreṇeva śikṣati ||

**8.49.2**

śatānīkeva pra jigāti dhṛṣṇuyā hanti vṛtrāṇi dāśuṣe | girer iva pra rasā asya pinvire datrāṇi purubhojasaḥ ||

**8.49.3**

ā tvā sutāsa indavo madā ya indra girvaṇaḥ | āpo na vajrinn anv okyaṁ saraḥ pṛṇanti śūra rādhase ||

**8.49.4**

anehansaṁ prataraṇaṁ vivakṣaṇam adhvaḥ svādiṣṭham īm piba | ā yathā mandasānaḥ kirāsi naḥ pra kṣudreva tmanā dhṛṣat ||

**8.49.5**

ā naḥ stomam upa dravad dhiyāno aśvo na sotṛbhiḥ | yaṁ te svadhāvan svadayanti dhenava indra kaṇveṣu rātayaḥ ||

**8.49.6**

ugraṁ na vīraṁ namasopa sedima vibhūtim akṣitāvasum | udrīva vajrinn avato na siñcate kṣarantīndra dhītayaḥ ||

**8.49.7**

yad dha nūnaṁ yad vā yajñe yad vā pṛthivyām adhi | ato no yajñam āśubhir mahemata ugra uGrebhir ā gahi ||

**8.49.8**

ajirāso harayo ye ta āśavo vātā iva prasak ṣiṇaḥ | yebhir apatyam manuṣaḥ parīyase yebhir viśvaṁ svar dṛśe ||

**8.49.9**

etāvatas ta īmaha indra sumnasya gomataḥ | yathā prāvo maghavan medhyātithiṁ yathā nīpātithiṁ dhane ||

**8.49.10**

yathā kaṇve maghavan trasadasyavi yathā pakthe daśavraje | yathā gośarye asanor ṛjiśvanī ndra gomad dhiraṇyavat ||

**8.50.1**

pra su śrutaṁ surādhasam arcā śakram abhiṣṭaye | yaḥ sunvate stuvate kāmyaṁ vasu sahasreṇeva maṁhate ||

**8.50.2**

śatānīkā hetayo asya duṣṭarā indrasya samiṣo mahīḥ | girir na bhujmā maghava tsu pinvate yad īṁ sutā amandiṣuḥ ||

**8.50.3**

yad īṁ sutāsa indavo 'bhi priyam amandiṣuḥ | āpo na dhāyi savanam ma ā vaso dughā ivopa dāśuṣe ||

**8.50.4**

anehansaṁ vo havamānam ūtaye madhvaḥ kṣaranti dhītayaḥ | ā tvā vaso havamānāsa indava upa streṣu dadhire ||

**8.50.5**

ā naḥ some svadhvara iyāno atyo na tośate | yaṁ te svadāvan svadanti gūrtayaḥ paure chandayase havam ||

**8.50.6**

pra vīram ugraṁ viviciṁ dhana spṛtaṁ vibhūtiṁ rādhaso mahaḥ | udrīva vajrinn avato vasutvanā sadā pīpetha dāśuṣe ||

**8.50.7**

yad dha nūnam parāvati yad vā pṛthivyāṁ divi | yujāna indra haribhir mahemata ṛṣva ṛṣvebhir ā gahi ||

**8.50.8**

rathirāso harayo ye te asridha ojo vātasya piprati | yebhir ni daśyum manuṣo nighoṣayo yebhiḥ svaḥ parīyase ||

**8.50.9**

etāvatas te vaso vidyāma śūra navyasaḥ | yathā prāva etaśaṁ kṛtvye dhane yathā vaśaṁ daśavraje ||

**8.50.10**

yathā kaṇve maghavan medhe adhvare dīrghanīthe damūnasi | yathā gośarye asiṣāso adrivo mayi gotraṁ hariśriyam ||

**8.51.1**

yathā̱ manau̱ sāṁva̍raṇau̱ soma̍m i̱ndrāpi̍baḥ su̱tam | nīpā̍tithau maghava̱n medhyā̍tithau̱ puṣṭi̍gau̱ śruṣṭi̍gau̱ sacā̍ ||

**8.51.2**

pā̱rṣa̱dvā̱ṇaḥ praska̍ṇva̱ṁ sam a̍sādaya̱c chayā̍na̱ṁ jivri̱m uddhi̍tam | sa̱hasrā̍ṇy asiṣāsa̱d gavā̱m ṛṣi̱s tvoto̱ dasya̍ve̱ vṛka̍ḥ ||

**8.51.3**

ya u̱kthebhi̱r na vi̱ndhate̍ ci̱kid ya ṛ̍ṣi̱coda̍naḥ | indra̱ṁ tam acchā̍ vada̱ navya̍syā ma̱ty ari̍ṣyanta̱ṁ na bhoja̍se ||

**8.51.4**

yasmā̍ a̱rkaṁ sa̱ptaśī̍rṣāṇam ānṛ̱cus tri̱dhātu̍m utta̱me pa̱de | sa tv i1̱̍mā viśvā̱ bhuva̍nāni cikrada̱d ād ij ja̍niṣṭa̱ pauṁsya̍m ||

**8.51.5**

yo no̍ dā̱tā vasū̍nā̱m indra̱ṁ taṁ hū̍mahe va̱yam | vi̱dmā hy a̍sya suma̱tiṁ navī̍yasīṁ ga̱mema̱ goma̍ti vra̱je ||

**8.51.6**

yasmai̱ tvaṁ va̍so dā̱nāya̱ śikṣa̍si̱ sa rā̱yas poṣa̍m aśnute | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yam ma̍ghavann indra girvaṇaḥ su̱tāva̍nto havāmahe ||

**8.51.7**

ka̱dā ca̱na sta̱rīr a̍si̱ nendra̍ saścasi dā̱śuṣe̍ | upo̱pen nu ma̍ghava̱n bhūya̱ in nu te̱ dāna̍ṁ de̱vasya̍ pṛcyate ||

**8.51.8**

pra yo na̍na̱kṣe a̱bhy oja̍sā̱ krivi̍ṁ va̱dhaiḥ śuṣṇa̍ṁ nigho̱ṣaya̍n | ya̱ded asta̍mbhīt pra̱thaya̍nn a̱mūṁ diva̱m ād ij ja̍niṣṭa̱ pārthi̍vaḥ ||

**8.51.9**

yasyā̱yaṁ viśva̱ āryo̱ dāsa̍ḥ śevadhi̱pā a̱riḥ | ti̱raś ci̍d a̱rye ruśa̍me̱ parī̍ravi̱ tubhyet so a̍jyate ra̱yiḥ ||

**8.51.10**

tu̱ra̱ṇyavo̱ madhu̍mantaṁ ghṛta̱ścuta̱ṁ viprā̍so a̱rkam ā̍nṛcuḥ | a̱sme ra̱yiḥ pa̍prathe̱ vṛṣṇya̱ṁ śavo̱ 'sme su̍vā̱nāsa̱ inda̍vaḥ ||

**8.52.1**

yathā̱ manau̱ viva̍svati̱ soma̍ṁ śa̱krāpi̍baḥ su̱tam | yathā̍ tri̱te chanda̍ indra̱ jujo̍ṣasy ā̱yau mā̍dayase̱ sacā̍ ||

**8.52.2**

pṛṣa̍dhre̱ medhye̍ māta̱riśva̱nīndra̍ suvā̱ne ama̍ndathāḥ | yathā̱ soma̱ṁ daśa̍śipre̱ daśo̍ṇye̱ syūma̍raśmā̱v ṛjū̍nasi ||

**8.52.3**

ya u̱kthā keva̍lā da̱dhe yaḥ soma̍ṁ dhṛṣi̱tāpi̍bat | yasmai̱ viṣṇu̱s trīṇi̍ pa̱dā vi̍cakra̱ma upa̍ mi̱trasya̱ dharma̍bhiḥ ||

**8.52.4**

yasya̱ tvam i̍ndra̱ stome̍ṣu cā̱kano̱ vāje̍ vājiñ chatakrato | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ su̱dughā̍m iva go̱duho̍ juhū̱masi̍ śrava̱syava̍ḥ ||

**8.52.5**

yo no̍ dā̱tā sa na̍ḥ pi̱tā ma̱hām̐ u̱gra ī̍śāna̱kṛt | ayā̍mann u̱gro ma̱ghavā̍ purū̱vasu̱r gor aśva̍sya̱ pra dā̍tu naḥ ||

**8.52.6**

yasmai̱ tvaṁ va̍so dā̱nāya̱ maṁha̍se̱ sa rā̱yas poṣa̍m invati | va̱sū̱yavo̱ vasu̍patiṁ śa̱takra̍tu̱ṁ stomai̱r indra̍ṁ havāmahe ||

**8.52.7**

ka̱dā ca̱na pra yu̍cchasy u̱bhe ni pā̍si̱ janma̍nī | turī̍yāditya̱ hava̍naṁ ta indri̱yam ā ta̍sthāv a̱mṛta̍ṁ di̱vi ||

**8.52.8**

yasmai̱ tvam ma̍ghavann indra girvaṇa̱ḥ śikṣo̱ śikṣa̍si dā̱śuṣe̍ | a̱smāka̱ṁ gira̍ u̱ta su̍ṣṭu̱tiṁ va̍so kaṇva̱vac chṛ̍ṇudhī̱ hava̍m ||

**8.52.9**

astā̍vi̱ manma̍ pū̱rvyam brahmendrā̍ya vocata | pū̱rvīr ṛ̱tasya̍ bṛha̱tīr a̍nūṣata sto̱tur me̱dhā a̍sṛkṣata ||

**8.52.10**

sam indro̱ rāyo̍ bṛha̱tīr a̍dhūnuta̱ saṁ kṣo̱ṇī sam u̱ sūrya̍m | saṁ śu̱krāsa̱ḥ śuca̍ya̱ḥ saṁ gavāśira̱ḥ somā̱ indra̍m amandiṣuḥ ||

**8.53.1**

u̱pa̱maṁ tvā̍ ma̱ghonā̱ṁ jyeṣṭha̍ṁ ca vṛṣa̱bhāṇā̍m | pū̱rbhitta̍mam maghavann indra go̱vida̱m īśā̍naṁ rā̱ya ī̍mahe ||

**8.53.2**

ya ā̱yuṁ kutsa̍m atithi̱gvam arda̍yo vāvṛdhā̱no di̱ve-di̍ve | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ harya̍śvaṁ śa̱takra̍tuṁ vāja̱yanto̍ havāmahe ||

**8.53.3**

ā no̱ viśve̍ṣā̱ṁ rasa̱m madhva̍ḥ siñca̱ntv adra̍yaḥ | ye pa̍rā̱vati̍ sunvi̱re jane̱ṣv ā ye a̍rvā̱vatīnda̍vaḥ ||

**8.53.4**

viśvā̱ dveṣā̍ṁsi ja̱hi cāva̱ cā kṛ̍dhi̱ viśve̍ sanva̱ntv ā vasu̍ | śīṣṭe̍ṣu cit te madi̱rāso̍ a̱ṁśavo̱ yatrā̱ soma̍sya tṛ̱mpasi̍ ||

**8.53.5**

indra̱ nedī̍ya̱ ed i̍hi mi̱tame̍dhābhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | ā śa̍ṁtama̱ śaṁta̍mābhir a̱bhiṣṭi̍bhi̱r ā svā̍pe svā̱pibhi̍ḥ ||

**8.53.6**

ā̱ji̱tura̱ṁ satpa̍tiṁ vi̱śvaca̍rṣaṇiṁ kṛ̱dhi pra̱jāsv ābha̍gam | pra sū ti̍rā̱ śacī̍bhi̱r ye ta̍ u̱kthina̱ḥ kratu̍m puna̱ta ā̍nu̱ṣak ||

**8.53.7**

yas te̱ sādhi̱ṣṭho 'va̍se̱ te syā̍ma̱ bhare̍ṣu te | va̱yaṁ hotrā̍bhir u̱ta de̱vahū̍tibhiḥ sasa̱vāṁso̍ manāmahe ||

**8.53.8**

a̱haṁ hi te̍ harivo̱ brahma̍ vāja̱yur ā̱jiṁ yāmi̱ sado̱tibhi̍ḥ | tvām id e̱va tam ame̱ sam a̍śva̱yur ga̱vyur agre̍ mathī̱nām ||

**8.54.1**

e̱tat ta̍ indra vī̱rya̍ṁ gī̱rbhir gṛ̱ṇanti̍ kā̱rava̍ḥ | te stobha̍nta̱ ūrja̍m āvan ghṛta̱ścuta̍m pau̱rāso̍ nakṣan dhī̱tibhi̍ḥ ||

**8.54.2**

nakṣa̍nta̱ indra̱m ava̍se sukṛ̱tyayā̱ yeṣā̍ṁ su̱teṣu̱ manda̍se | yathā̍ saṁva̱rte ama̍do̱ yathā̍ kṛ̱śa e̱vāsme i̍ndra matsva ||

**8.54.3**

ā no̱ viśve̍ sa̱joṣa̍so̱ devā̍so̱ ganta̱nopa̍ naḥ | vasa̍vo ru̱drā ava̍se na̱ ā ga̍mañ chṛ̱ṇvantu̍ ma̱ruto̱ hava̍m ||

**8.54.4**

pū̱ṣā viṣṇu̱r hava̍nam me̱ sara̍sva̱ty ava̍ntu sa̱pta sindha̍vaḥ | āpo̱ vāta̱ḥ parva̍tāso̱ vana̱spati̍ḥ śṛ̱ṇotu̍ pṛthi̱vī hava̍m ||

**8.54.5**

yad i̍ndra̱ rādho̱ asti̍ te̱ māgho̍nam maghavattama | tena̍ no bodhi sadha̱mādyo̍ vṛ̱dhe bhago̍ dā̱nāya̍ vṛtrahan ||

**8.54.6**

āji̍pate nṛpate̱ tvam id dhi no̱ vāja̱ ā va̍kṣi sukrato | vī̱tī hotrā̍bhir u̱ta de̱vavī̍tibhiḥ sasa̱vāṁso̱ vi śṛ̍ṇvire ||

**8.54.7**

santi̱ hy a1̱̍rya ā̱śiṣa̱ indra̱ āyu̱r janā̍nām | a̱smān na̍kṣasva maghava̱nn upāva̍se dhu̱kṣasva̍ pi̱pyuṣī̱m iṣa̍m ||

**8.54.8**

va̱yaṁ ta̍ indra̱ stome̍bhir vidhema̱ tvam a̱smāka̍ṁ śatakrato | mahi̍ sthū̱raṁ śa̍śa̱yaṁ rādho̱ ahra̍ya̱m praska̍ṇvāya̱ ni to̍śaya ||

**8.55.1**

bhūrīd indra̍sya vī̱rya1̱̍ṁ vy akhya̍m a̱bhy āya̍ti | rādha̍s te dasyave vṛka ||

**8.55.2**

śa̱taṁ śve̱tāsa̍ u̱kṣaṇo̍ di̱vi tāro̱ na ro̍cante | ma̱hnā diva̱ṁ na ta̍stabhuḥ ||

**8.55.3**

śa̱taṁ ve̱ṇūñ cha̱taṁ śuna̍ḥ śa̱taṁ carmā̍ṇi mlā̱tāni̍ | śa̱tam me̍ balbajastu̱kā aru̍ṣīṇā̱ṁ catu̍ḥśatam ||

**8.55.4**

su̱de̱vāḥ stha̍ kāṇvāyanā̱ vayo̍-vayo vica̱ranta̍ḥ | aśvā̍so̱ na ca̍ṅkramata ||

**8.55.5**

ād it sā̱ptasya̍ carkira̱nn ānū̍nasya̱ mahi̱ śrava̍ḥ | śyāvī̍r atidhva̱san pa̱thaś cakṣu̍ṣā ca̱na sa̱ṁnaśe̍ ||

**8.56.1**

prati̍ te dasyave vṛka̱ rādho̍ ada̱rśy ahra̍yam | dyaur na pra̍thi̱nā śava̍ḥ ||

**8.56.2**

daśa̱ mahya̍m pautakra̱taḥ sa̱hasrā̱ dasya̍ve̱ vṛka̍ḥ ||

**8.56.3**

śa̱tam me̍ garda̱bhānā̍ṁ śa̱tam ūrṇā̍vatīnām | śa̱taṁ dā̱sām̐ ati̱ sraja̍ḥ ||

**8.56.4**

tatro̱ api̱ prāṇī̍yata pū̱takra̍tāyai̱ vya̍ktā | aśvā̍nā̱m in na yū̱thyā̍m ||

**8.56.5**

ace̍ty a̱gniś ci̍ki̱tur ha̍vya̱vāṭ sa su̱madra̍thaḥ | a̱gniḥ śu̱kreṇa̍ śo̱ciṣā̍ bṛ̱hat sūro̍ arocata di̱vi sūryo̍ arocata ||

**8.57.1**

yu̱vaṁ de̍vā̱ kratu̍nā pū̱rvyeṇa̍ yu̱ktā rathe̍na tavi̱ṣaṁ ya̍jatrā | āga̍cchataṁ nāsatyā̱ śacī̍bhir i̱daṁ tṛ̱tīya̱ṁ sava̍nam pibāthaḥ ||

**8.57.2**

yu̱vāṁ de̱vās traya̍ ekāda̱śāsa̍ḥ sa̱tyāḥ sa̱tyasya̍ dadṛśe pu̱rastā̍t | a̱smāka̍ṁ ya̱jñaṁ sava̍naṁ juṣā̱ṇā pā̱taṁ soma̍m aśvinā̱ dīdya̍gnī ||

**8.57.3**

pa̱nāyya̱ṁ tad a̍śvinā kṛ̱taṁ vā̍ṁ vṛṣa̱bho di̱vo raja̍saḥ pṛthi̱vyāḥ | sa̱hasra̱ṁ śaṁsā̍ u̱ta ye gavi̍ṣṭau̱ sarvā̱m̐ it tām̐ upa̍ yātā̱ piba̍dhyai ||

**8.57.4**

a̱yaṁ vā̍m bhā̱go nihi̍to yajatre̱mā giro̍ nāsa̱tyopa̍ yātam | piba̍ta̱ṁ soma̱m madhu̍mantam a̱sme pra dā̱śvāṁsa̍m avata̱ṁ śacī̍bhiḥ ||

**8.58.1**

yam ṛ̱tvijo̍ bahu̱dhā ka̱lpaya̍nta̱ḥ sace̍taso ya̱jñam i̱maṁ vaha̍nti | yo a̍nūcā̱no brā̍hma̱ṇo yu̱kta ā̍sī̱t kā svi̱t tatra̱ yaja̍mānaśya sa̱ṁvit ||

**8.58.2**

eka̍ e̱vāgnir ba̍hu̱dhā sami̍ddha̱ eka̱ḥ sūryo̱ viśva̱m anu̱ prabhū̍taḥ | ekai̱voṣāḥ sarva̍m i̱daṁ vi bhā̱ty eka̱ṁ vā i̱daṁ vi ba̍bhūva̱ sarva̍m ||

**8.58.3**

jyoti̍ṣmantaṁ ketu̱manta̍ṁ trica̱kraṁ su̱khaṁ ratha̍ṁ su̱ṣada̱m bhūri̍vāram | ci̱trāma̍ghā̱ yasya̱ yoge̍ 'dhijajñe̱ taṁ vā̍ṁ hu̱ve ati̍ rikta̱m piba̍dhyai ||

**8.59.1**

i̱māni̍ vām bhāga̱dheyā̍ni sisrata̱ indrā̍varuṇā̱ pra ma̱he su̱teṣu̍ vām | ya̱jñe-ya̍jñe ha̱ sava̍nā bhura̱ṇyatho̱ yat su̍nva̱te yaja̍mānaśya̱ śikṣa̍thaḥ ||

**8.59.2**

ni̱ṣṣidhva̍rī̱r oṣa̍dhī̱r āpa̍ āstā̱m indrā̍varuṇā mahi̱māna̱m āśa̍ta | yā sisra̍tū̱ raja̍saḥ pā̱re adhva̍no̱ yayo̱ḥ śatru̱r naki̱r āde̍va̱ oha̍te ||

**8.59.3**

sa̱tyaṁ tad i̍ndrāvaruṇā kṛ̱śasya̍ vā̱m madhva̍ ū̱rmiṁ du̍hate sa̱pta vāṇī̍ḥ | tābhi̍r dā̱śvāṁsa̍m avataṁ śubhas patī̱ yo vā̱m ada̍bdho a̱bhi pāti̱ citti̍bhiḥ ||

**8.59.4**

ghṛ̱ta̱pruṣa̱ḥ saumyā̍ jī̱radā̍navaḥ sa̱pta svasā̍ra̱ḥ sada̍na ṛ̱tasya̍ | yā ha̍ vām indrāvaruṇā ghṛta̱ścuta̱s tābhi̍r dhatta̱ṁ yaja̍mānaśya śikṣatam ||

**8.59.5**

avo̍cāma maha̱te saubha̍gāya sa̱tyaṁ tve̱ṣābhyā̍m mahi̱māna̍m indri̱yam | a̱smān sv i̍ndrāvaruṇā ghṛta̱ścuta̱s tribhi̍ḥ sā̱ptebhi̍r avataṁ śubhas patī ||

**8.59.6**

indrā̍varuṇā̱ yad ṛ̱ṣibhyo̍ manī̱ṣāṁ vā̱co ma̱tiṁ śru̱tam a̍datta̱m agre̍ | yāni̱ sthānā̍ny asṛjanta̱ dhīrā̍ ya̱jñaṁ ta̍nvā̱nās tapa̍sā̱bhy a̍paśyam ||

**8.59.7**

indrā̍varuṇā saumana̱sam adṛ̍ptaṁ rā̱yas poṣa̱ṁ yaja̍māneṣu dhattam | pra̱jām pu̱ṣṭim bhū̍tim a̱smāsu̍ dhattaṁ dīrghāyu̱tvāya̱ pra ti̍rataṁ na̱ āyu̍ḥ ||

**8.60.1**

agna̱ ā yā̍hy a̱gnibhi̱r hotā̍raṁ tvā vṛṇīmahe | ā tvām a̍naktu̱ praya̍tā ha̱viṣma̍tī̱ yaji̍ṣṭham ba̱rhir ā̱sade̍ ||

**8.60.2**

acchā̱ hi tvā̍ sahasaḥ sūno aṅgira̱ḥ sruca̱ś cara̍nty adhva̱re | ū̱rjo napā̍taṁ ghṛ̱take̍śam īmahe̱ 'gniṁ ya̱jñeṣu̍ pū̱rvyam ||

**8.60.3**

agne̍ ka̱vir ve̍dhā a̍si̱ hotā̍ pāvaka̱ yakṣya̍ḥ | ma̱ndro yaji̍ṣṭho adhva̱reṣv īḍyo̱ vipre̍bhiḥ śukra̱ manma̍bhiḥ ||

**8.60.4**

adro̍gha̱m ā va̍hośa̱to ya̍viṣṭhya de̱vām̐ a̍jasra vī̱taye̍ | a̱bhi prayā̍ṁsi̱ sudhi̱tā va̍so gahi̱ manda̍sva dhī̱tibhi̍r hi̱taḥ ||

**8.60.5**

tvam it sa̱prathā̍ a̱sy agne̍ trātar ṛ̱tas ka̱viḥ | tvāṁ viprā̍saḥ samidhāna dīdi̱va ā vi̍vāsanti ve̱dhasa̍ḥ ||

**8.60.6**

śocā̍ śociṣṭha dīdi̱hi vi̱śe mayo̱ rāsva̍ sto̱tre ma̱hām̐ a̍si | de̱vānā̱ṁ śarma̱n mama̍ santu sū̱raya̍ḥ śatrū̱ṣāha̍ḥ sva̱gnaya̍ḥ ||

**8.60.7**

yathā̍ cid vṛ̱ddham a̍ta̱sam agne̍ sa̱ṁjūrva̍si̱ kṣami̍ | e̱vā da̍ha mitramaho̱ yo a̍sma̱dhrug du̱rmanmā̱ kaś ca̱ vena̍ti ||

**8.60.8**

mā no̱ martā̍ya ri̱pave̍ rakṣa̱svine̱ māghaśa̍ṁsāya rīradhaḥ | asre̍dhadbhis ta̱raṇi̍bhir yaviṣṭhya śi̱vebhi̍ḥ pāhi pā̱yubhi̍ḥ ||

**8.60.9**

pā̱hi no̍ agna̱ eka̍yā pā̱hy u1̱̍ta dvi̱tīya̍yā | pā̱hi gī̱rbhis ti̱sṛbhi̍r ūrjām pate pā̱hi ca̍ta̱sṛbhi̍r vaso ||

**8.60.10**

pā̱hi viśva̍smād ra̱kṣaso̱ arā̍vṇa̱ḥ pra sma̱ vāje̍ṣu no 'va | tvām id dhi nedi̍ṣṭhaṁ de̱vatā̍taya ā̱piṁ nakṣā̍mahe vṛ̱dhe ||

**8.60.11**

ā no̍ agne vayo̱vṛdha̍ṁ ra̱yim pā̍vaka̱ śaṁsya̍m | rāsvā̍ ca na upamāte puru̱spṛha̱ṁ sunī̍tī̱ svaya̍śastaram ||

**8.60.12**

yena̱ vaṁsā̍ma̱ pṛta̍nāsu̱ śardha̍ta̱s tara̍nto a̱rya ā̱diśa̍ḥ | sa tvaṁ no̍ vardha̱ praya̍sā śacīvaso̱ jinvā̱ dhiyo̍ vasu̱vida̍ḥ ||

**8.60.13**

śiśā̍no vṛṣa̱bho ya̍thā̱gniḥ śṛṅge̱ davi̍dhvat | ti̱gmā a̍sya̱ hana̍vo̱ na pra̍ti̱dhṛṣe̍ su̱jambha̱ḥ saha̍so ya̱huḥ ||

**8.60.14**

na̱hi te̍ agne vṛṣabha prati̱dhṛṣe̱ jambhā̍so̱ yad vi̱tiṣṭha̍se | sa tvaṁ no̍ hota̱ḥ suhu̍taṁ ha̱viṣ kṛ̍dhi̱ vaṁsvā̍ no̱ vāryā̍ pu̱ru ||

**8.60.15**

śeṣe̱ vane̍ṣu mā̱troḥ saṁ tvā̱ martā̍sa indhate | ata̍ndro ha̱vyā va̍hasi havi̱ṣkṛta̱ ād id de̱veṣu̍ rājasi ||

**8.60.16**

sa̱pta hotā̍ra̱s tam id ī̍ḻate̱ tvāgne̍ su̱tyaja̱m ahra̍yam | bhi̱natsy adri̱ṁ tapa̍sā̱ vi śo̱ciṣā̱ prāgne̍ tiṣṭha̱ janā̱m̐ ati̍ ||

**8.60.17**

a̱gnim-a̍gniṁ vo̱ adhri̍guṁ hu̱vema̍ vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣaḥ | a̱gniṁ hi̱tapra̍yasaḥ śaśva̱tīṣv ā hotā̍raṁ carṣaṇī̱nām ||

**8.60.18**

kete̍na̱ śarma̱n sacate suṣā̱maṇy agne̱ tubhya̍ṁ ciki̱tvanā̍ | i̱ṣa̱ṇyayā̍ naḥ puru̱rūpa̱m ā bha̍ra̱ vāja̱ṁ nedi̍ṣṭham ū̱taye̍ ||

**8.60.19**

agne̱ jari̍tar vi̱śpati̍s tepā̱no de̍va ra̱kṣasa̍ḥ | apro̍ṣivān gṛ̱hapa̍tir ma̱hām̐ a̍si di̱vas pā̱yur du̍roṇa̱yuḥ ||

**8.60.20**

mā no̱ rakṣa̱ ā ve̍śīd āghṛṇīvaso̱ mā yā̱tur yā̍tu̱māva̍tām | pa̱ro̱ga̱vyū̱ty ani̍rā̱m apa̱ kṣudha̱m agne̱ sedha̍ rakṣa̱svina̍ḥ ||

**8.61.1**

u̱bhaya̍ṁ śṛ̱ṇava̍c ca na̱ indro̍ a̱rvāg i̱daṁ vaca̍ḥ | sa̱trācyā̍ ma̱ghavā̱ soma̍pītaye dhi̱yā śavi̍ṣṭha̱ ā ga̍mat ||

**8.61.2**

taṁ hi sva̱rāja̍ṁ vṛṣa̱bhaṁ tam oja̍se dhi̱ṣaṇe̍ niṣṭata̱kṣatu̍ḥ | u̱topa̱mānā̍m pratha̱mo ni ṣī̍dasi̱ soma̍kāma̱ṁ hi te̱ mana̍ḥ ||

**8.61.3**

ā vṛ̍ṣasva purūvaso su̱tasye̱ndrāndha̍saḥ | vi̱dmā hi tvā̍ harivaḥ pṛ̱tsu sā̍sa̱him adhṛ̍ṣṭaṁ cid dadhṛ̱ṣvaṇi̍m ||

**8.61.4**

aprā̍misatya maghava̱n tathed a̍sa̱d indra̱ kratvā̱ yathā̱ vaśa̍ḥ | sa̱nema̱ vāja̱ṁ tava̍ śipri̱nn ava̍sā ma̱kṣū ci̱d yanto̍ adrivaḥ ||

**8.61.5**

śa̱gdhy ū̱3̱̍ ṣu śa̍cīpata̱ indra̱ viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | bhaga̱ṁ na hi tvā̍ ya̱śasa̍ṁ vasu̱vida̱m anu̍ śūra̱ carā̍masi ||

**8.61.6**

pau̱ro aśva̍sya puru̱kṛd gavā̍m a̱sy utso̍ deva hira̱ṇyaya̍ḥ | naki̱r hi dāna̍m pari̱mardhi̍ṣa̱t tve yad-ya̱d yāmi̱ tad ā bha̍ra ||

**8.61.7**

tvaṁ hy ehi̱ cera̍ve vi̱dā bhaga̱ṁ vasu̍ttaye | ud vā̍vṛṣasva maghava̱n gavi̍ṣṭaya̱ ud i̱ndrāśva̍miṣṭaye ||

**8.61.8**

tvam pu̱rū sa̱hasrā̍ṇi śa̱tāni̍ ca yū̱thā dā̱nāya̍ maṁhase | ā pu̍raṁda̱raṁ ca̍kṛma̱ vipra̍vacasa̱ indra̱ṁ gāya̱nto 'va̍se ||

**8.61.9**

a̱vi̱pro vā̱ yad avi̍dha̱d vipro̍ vendra te̱ vaca̍ḥ | sa pra ma̍mandat tvā̱yā śa̍takrato̱ prācā̍manyo̱ aha̍ṁsana ||

**8.61.10**

u̱grabā̍hur mrakṣa̱kṛtvā̍ puraṁda̱ro yadi̍ me śṛ̱ṇava̱d dhava̍m | va̱sū̱yavo̱ vasu̍patiṁ śa̱takra̍tu̱ṁ stomai̱r indra̍ṁ havāmahe ||

**8.61.11**

na pā̱pāso̍ manāmahe̱ nārā̍yāso̱ na jaḻha̍vaḥ | yad in nv indra̱ṁ vṛṣa̍ṇa̱ṁ sacā̍ su̱te sakhā̍yaṁ kṛ̱ṇavā̍mahai ||

**8.61.12**

u̱graṁ yu̍yujma̱ pṛta̍nāsu sāsa̱him ṛ̱ṇakā̍ti̱m adā̍bhyam | vedā̍ bhṛ̱maṁ ci̱t sani̍tā ra̱thīta̍mo vā̱jina̱ṁ yam id ū̱ naśa̍t ||

**8.61.13**

yata̍ indra̱ bhayā̍mahe̱ tato̍ no̱ abha̍yaṁ kṛdhi | magha̍vañ cha̱gdhi tava̱ tan na̍ ū̱tibhi̱r vi dviṣo̱ vi mṛdho̍ jahi ||

**8.61.14**

tvaṁ hi rā̍dhaspate̱ rādha̍so ma̱haḥ kṣaya̱syāsi̍ vidha̱taḥ | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yam ma̍ghavann indra girvaṇaḥ su̱tāva̍nto havāmahe ||

**8.61.15**

indra̱ḥ spaḻ u̱ta vṛ̍tra̱hā pa̍ra̱spā no̱ vare̍ṇyaḥ | sa no̍ rakṣiṣac cara̱maṁ sa ma̍dhya̱maṁ sa pa̱ścāt pā̍tu naḥ pu̱raḥ ||

**8.61.16**

tvaṁ na̍ḥ pa̱ścād a̍dha̱rād u̍tta̱rāt pu̱ra indra̱ ni pā̍hi vi̱śvata̍ḥ | ā̱re a̱smat kṛ̍ṇuhi̱ daivya̍m bha̱yam ā̱re he̱tīr ade̍vīḥ ||

**8.61.17**

a̱dyādyā̱ śvaḥ-śva̱ indra̱ trāsva̍ pa̱re ca̍ naḥ | viśvā̍ ca no jari̱tṝn sa̍tpate̱ ahā̱ divā̱ nakta̍ṁ ca rakṣiṣaḥ ||

**8.61.18**

pra̱bha̱ṅgī śūro̍ ma̱ghavā̍ tu̱vīma̍gha̱ḥ sammi̍ślo vi̱ryā̍ya̱ kam | u̱bhā te̍ bā̱hū vṛṣa̍ṇā śatakrato̱ ni yā vajra̍m mimi̱kṣatu̍ḥ ||

**8.62.1**

pro a̍smā̱ upa̍stuti̱m bhara̍tā̱ yaj jujo̍ṣati | u̱kthair indra̍sya̱ māhi̍na̱ṁ vayo̍ vardhanti so̱mino̍ bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.2**

a̱yu̱jo asa̍mo̱ nṛbhi̱r eka̍ḥ kṛ̱ṣṭīr a̱yāsya̍ḥ | pū̱rvīr ati̱ pra vā̍vṛdhe̱ viśvā̍ jā̱tāny oja̍sā bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.3**

ahi̍tena ci̱d arva̍tā jī̱radā̍nuḥ siṣāsati | pra̱vācya̍m indra̱ tat tava̍ vī̱ryā̍ṇi kariṣya̱to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.4**

ā yā̍hi kṛ̱ṇavā̍ma ta̱ indra̱ brahmā̍ṇi̱ vardha̍nā | yebhi̍ḥ śaviṣṭha cā̱kano̍ bha̱dram i̱ha śra̍vasya̱te bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.5**

dhṛ̱ṣa̱taś ci̍d dhṛ̱ṣan mana̍ḥ kṛ̱ṇoṣī̍ndra̱ yat tvam | tī̱vraiḥ somai̍ḥ saparya̱to namo̍bhiḥ prati̱bhūṣa̍to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.6**

ava̍ caṣṭa̱ ṛcī̍ṣamo 'va̱tām̐ i̍va̱ mānu̍ṣaḥ | ju̱ṣṭvī dakṣa̍sya so̱mina̱ḥ sakhā̍yaṁ kṛṇute̱ yuja̍m bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.7**

viśve̍ ta indra vī̱rya̍ṁ de̱vā anu̱ kratu̍ṁ daduḥ | bhuvo̱ viśva̍sya̱ gopa̍tiḥ puruṣṭuta bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.8**

gṛ̱ṇe tad i̍ndra te̱ śava̍ upa̱maṁ de̱vatā̍taye | yad dhaṁsi̍ vṛ̱tram oja̍sā śacīpate bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.9**

sama̍neva vapuṣya̱taḥ kṛ̱ṇava̱n mānu̍ṣā yu̱gā | vi̱de tad indra̱ś ceta̍na̱m adha̍ śru̱to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.10**

uj jā̱tam i̍ndra te̱ śava̱ ut tvām ut tava̱ kratu̍m | bhūri̍go̱ bhūri̍ vāvṛdhu̱r magha̍va̱n tava̱ śarma̍ṇi bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.11**

a̱haṁ ca̱ tvaṁ ca̍ vṛtraha̱n saṁ yu̍jyāva sa̱nibhya̱ ā | a̱rā̱tī̱vā ci̍d adri̱vo 'nu̍ nau śūra maṁsate bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.62.12**

sa̱tyam id vā u̱ taṁ va̱yam indra̍ṁ stavāma̱ nānṛ̍tam | ma̱hām̐ asu̍nvato va̱dho bhūri̱ jyotī̍ṁṣi sunva̱to bha̱drā indra̍sya rā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.63.1**

sa pū̱rvyo ma̱hānā̍ṁ ve̱naḥ kratu̍bhir ānaje | yasya̱ dvārā̱ manu̍ṣ pi̱tā de̱veṣu̱ dhiya̍ āna̱je ||

**8.63.2**

di̱vo māna̱ṁ not sa̍da̱n soma̍pṛṣṭhāso̱ adra̍yaḥ | u̱kthā brahma̍ ca̱ śaṁsyā̍ ||

**8.63.3**

sa vi̱dvām̐ aṅgi̍robhya̱ indro̱ gā a̍vṛṇo̱d apa̍ | stu̱ṣe tad a̍sya̱ pauṁsya̍m ||

**8.63.4**

sa pra̱tnathā̍ kavivṛ̱dha indro̍ vā̱kasya̍ va̱kṣaṇi̍ḥ | śi̱vo a̱rkasya̱ homa̍ny asma̱trā ga̱ntv ava̍se ||

**8.63.5**

ād ū̱ nu te̱ anu̱ kratu̱ṁ svāhā̱ vara̍sya̱ yajya̍vaḥ | śvā̱tram a̱rkā a̍nūṣa̱tendra̍ go̱trasya̍ dā̱vane̍ ||

**8.63.6**

indre̱ viśvā̍ni vī̱ryā̍ kṛ̱tāni̱ kartvā̍ni ca | yam a̱rkā a̍dhva̱raṁ vi̱duḥ ||

**8.63.7**

yat pāñca̍janyayā vi̱śendre̱ ghoṣā̱ asṛ̍kṣata | astṛ̍ṇād ba̱rhaṇā̍ vi̱po̱3̱̍ 'ryo māna̍sya̱ sa kṣaya̍ḥ ||

**8.63.8**

i̱yam u̍ te̱ anu̍ṣṭutiś cakṛ̱ṣe tāni̱ pauṁsyā̍ | prāva̍ś ca̱krasya̍ varta̱nim ||

**8.63.9**

a̱sya vṛṣṇo̱ vyoda̍na u̱ru kra̍miṣṭa jī̱vase̍ | yava̱ṁ na pa̱śva ā da̍de ||

**8.63.10**

tad dadhā̍nā ava̱syavo̍ yu̱ṣmābhi̱r dakṣa̍pitaraḥ | syāma̍ ma̱rutva̍to vṛ̱dhe ||

**8.63.11**

baḻ ṛ̱tviyā̍ya̱ dhāmna̱ ṛkva̍bhiḥ śūra nonumaḥ | jeṣā̍mendra̱ tvayā̍ yu̱jā ||

**8.63.12**

a̱sme ru̱drā me̱hanā̱ parva̍tāso vṛtra̱hatye̱ bhara̍hūtau sa̱joṣā̍ḥ | yaḥ śaṁsa̍te stuva̱te dhāyi̍ pa̱jra indra̍jyeṣṭhā a̱smām̐ a̍vantu de̱vāḥ ||

**8.64.1**

ut tvā̍ mandantu̱ stomā̍ḥ kṛṇu̱ṣva rādho̍ adrivaḥ | ava̍ brahma̱dviṣo̍ jahi ||

**8.64.2**

pa̱dā pa̱ṇīm̐r a̍rā̱dhaso̱ ni bā̍dhasva ma̱hām̐ a̍si | na̱hi tvā̱ kaś ca̱na prati̍ ||

**8.64.3**

tvam ī̍śiṣe su̱tānā̱m indra̱ tvam asu̍tānām | tvaṁ rājā̱ janā̍nām ||

**8.64.4**

ehi̱ prehi̱ kṣayo̍ di̱vy ā̱3̱̍ghoṣa̍ñ carṣaṇī̱nām | obhe pṛ̍ṇāsi̱ roda̍sī ||

**8.64.5**

tyaṁ ci̱t parva̍taṁ gi̱riṁ śa̱tava̍ntaṁ saha̱sriṇa̍m | vi sto̱tṛbhyo̍ rurojitha ||

**8.64.6**

va̱yam u̍ tvā̱ divā̍ su̱te va̱yaṁ nakta̍ṁ havāmahe | a̱smāka̱ṁ kāma̱m ā pṛ̍ṇa ||

**8.64.7**

kva1̱̍ sya vṛ̍ṣa̱bho yuvā̍ tuvi̱grīvo̱ anā̍nataḥ | bra̱hmā kas taṁ sa̍paryati ||

**8.64.8**

kasya̍ svi̱t sava̍na̱ṁ vṛṣā̍ juju̱ṣvām̐ ava̍ gacchati | indra̱ṁ ka u̍ svi̱d ā ca̍ke ||

**8.64.9**

kaṁ te̍ dā̱nā a̍sakṣata̱ vṛtra̍ha̱n kaṁ su̱vīryā̍ | u̱kthe ka u̍ svi̱d anta̍maḥ ||

**8.64.10**

a̱yaṁ te̱ mānu̍ṣe̱ jane̱ soma̍ḥ pū̱ruṣu̍ sūyate | tasyehi̱ pra dra̍vā̱ piba̍ ||

**8.64.11**

a̱yaṁ te̍ śarya̱ṇāva̍ti su̱ṣomā̍yā̱m adhi̍ pri̱yaḥ | ā̱rjī̱kīye̍ ma̱dinta̍maḥ ||

**8.64.12**

tam a̱dya rādha̍se ma̱he cāru̱m madā̍ya̱ ghṛṣva̍ye | ehī̍m indra̱ dravā̱ piba̍ ||

**8.65.1**

yad i̍ndra̱ prāg apā̱g uda̱ṅ nya̍g vā hū̱yase̱ nṛbhi̍ḥ | ā yā̍hi̱ tūya̍m ā̱śubhi̍ḥ ||

**8.65.2**

yad vā̍ pra̱srava̍ṇe di̱vo mā̱dayā̍se̱ sva̍rṇare | yad vā̍ samu̱dre andha̍saḥ ||

**8.65.3**

ā tvā̍ gī̱rbhir ma̱hām u̱ruṁ hu̱ve gām i̍va̱ bhoja̍se | indra̱ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||

**8.65.4**

ā ta̍ indra mahi̱māna̱ṁ hara̍yo deva te̱ maha̍ḥ | rathe̍ vahantu̱ bibhra̍taḥ ||

**8.65.5**

indra̍ gṛṇī̱ṣa u̍ stu̱ṣe ma̱hām̐ u̱gra ī̍śāna̱kṛt | ehi̍ naḥ su̱tam piba̍ ||

**8.65.6**

su̱tāva̍ntas tvā va̱yam praya̍svanto havāmahe | i̱daṁ no̍ ba̱rhir ā̱sade̍ ||

**8.65.7**

yac ci̱d dhi śaśva̍tā̱m asīndra̱ sādhā̍raṇa̱s tvam | taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ ha̍vāmahe ||

**8.65.8**

i̱daṁ te̍ so̱myam madhv adhu̍kṣa̱nn adri̍bhi̱r nara̍ḥ | ju̱ṣā̱ṇa i̍ndra̱ tat pi̍ba ||

**8.65.9**

viśvā̍m̐ a̱ryo vi̍pa̱ścito 'ti̍ khya̱s tūya̱m ā ga̍hi | a̱sme dhe̍hi̱ śravo̍ bṛ̱hat ||

**8.65.10**

dā̱tā me̱ pṛṣa̍tīnā̱ṁ rājā̍ hiraṇya̱vīnā̍m | mā de̍vā ma̱ghavā̍ riṣat ||

**8.65.11**

sa̱hasre̱ pṛṣa̍tīnā̱m adhi̍ śca̱ndram bṛ̱hat pṛ̱thu | śu̱kraṁ hira̍ṇya̱m ā da̍de ||

**8.65.12**

napā̍to du̱rgaha̍sya me sa̱hasre̍ṇa su̱rādha̍saḥ | śravo̍ de̱veṣv a̍krata ||

**8.66.1**

taro̍bhir vo vi̱dadva̍su̱m indra̍ṁ sa̱bādha̍ ū̱taye̍ | bṛ̱had gāya̍ntaḥ su̱taso̍me adhva̱re hu̱ve bhara̱ṁ na kā̱riṇa̍m ||

**8.66.2**

na yaṁ du̱dhrā vara̍nte̱ na sthi̱rā muro̱ made̍ suśi̱pram andha̍saḥ | ya ā̱dṛtyā̍ śaśamā̱nāya̍ sunva̱te dātā̍ jari̱tra u̱kthya̍m ||

**8.66.3**

yaḥ śa̱kro mṛ̱kṣo aśvyo̱ yo vā̱ kījo̍ hira̱ṇyaya̍ḥ | sa ū̱rvasya̍ rejaya̱ty apā̍vṛti̱m indro̱ gavya̍sya vṛtra̱hā ||

**8.66.4**

nikhā̍taṁ ci̱d yaḥ pu̍rusambhṛ̱taṁ vasūd id vapa̍ti dā̱śuṣe̍ | va̱jrī su̍śi̱pro harya̍śva̱ it ka̍ra̱d indra̱ḥ kratvā̱ yathā̱ vaśa̍t ||

**8.66.5**

yad vā̱vantha̍ puruṣṭuta pu̱rā ci̍c chūra nṛ̱ṇām | va̱yaṁ tat ta̍ indra̱ sam bha̍rāmasi ya̱jñam u̱kthaṁ tu̱raṁ vaca̍ḥ ||

**8.66.6**

sacā̱ some̍ṣu puruhūta vajrivo̱ madā̍ya dyukṣa somapāḥ ||

**8.66.7**

va̱yam e̍nam i̱dā hyo 'pī̍peme̱ha va̱jriṇa̍m | tasmā̍ u a̱dya sa̍ma̱nā su̱tam bha̱rā nū̱nam bhū̍ṣata śru̱te ||

**8.66.8**

vṛka̍ś cid asya vāra̱ṇa u̍rā̱mathi̱r ā va̱yune̍ṣu bhūṣati | semaṁ na̱ḥ stoma̍ṁ jujuṣā̱ṇa ā ga̱hīndra̱ pra ci̱trayā̍ dhi̱yā ||

**8.66.9**

kad ū̱ nv a1̱̍syākṛ̍ta̱m indra̍syāsti̱ pauṁsya̍m | keno̱ nu ka̱ṁ śroma̍tena̱ na śu̍śruve ja̱nuṣa̱ḥ pari̍ vṛtra̱hā ||

**8.66.10**

kad ū̍ ma̱hīr adhṛ̍ṣṭā asya̱ tavi̍ṣī̱ḥ kad u̍ vṛtra̱ghno astṛ̍tam | indro̱ viśvān̍ beka̱nāṭā̍m̐ aha̱rdṛśa̍ u̱ta kratvā̍ pa̱ṇīm̐r a̱bhi ||

**8.66.11**

va̱yaṁ ghā̍ te̱ apū̱rvyendra̱ brahmā̍ṇi vṛtrahan | pu̱rū̱tamā̍saḥ puruhūta vajrivo bhṛ̱tiṁ na pra bha̍rāmasi ||

**8.66.12**

pū̱rvīś ci̱d dhi tve tu̍vikūrminn ā̱śaso̱ hava̍nta indro̱taya̍ḥ | ti̱raś ci̍d a̱ryaḥ sava̱nā va̍so gahi̱ śavi̍ṣṭha śru̱dhi me̱ hava̍m ||

**8.66.13**

va̱yaṁ ghā̍ te̱ tve id v indra̱ viprā̱ api̍ ṣmasi | na̱hi tvad a̱nyaḥ pu̍ruhūta̱ kaś ca̱na magha̍va̱nn asti̍ marḍi̱tā ||

**8.66.14**

tvaṁ no̍ a̱syā ama̍ter u̱ta kṣu̱dho̱3̱̍ 'bhiśa̍ste̱r ava̍ spṛdhi | tvaṁ na̍ ū̱tī tava̍ ci̱trayā̍ dhi̱yā śikṣā̍ śaciṣṭha gātu̱vit ||

**8.66.15**

soma̱ id va̍ḥ su̱to a̍stu̱ kala̍yo̱ mā bi̍bhītana | aped e̱ṣa dhva̱smāya̍ti sva̱yaṁ ghai̱ṣo apā̍yati ||

**8.67.1**

tyān nu kṣa̱triyā̱m̐ ava̍ ādi̱tyān yā̍ciṣāmahe | su̱mṛ̱ḻī̱kām̐ a̱bhiṣṭa̍ye ||

**8.67.2**

mi̱tro no̱ aty a̍ṁha̱tiṁ varu̍ṇaḥ parṣad arya̱mā | ā̱di̱tyāso̱ yathā̍ vi̱duḥ ||

**8.67.3**

teṣā̱ṁ hi ci̱tram u̱kthya1̱̍ṁ varū̍tha̱m asti̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ | ā̱di̱tyānā̍m ara̱ṁkṛte̍ ||

**8.67.4**

mahi̍ vo maha̱tām avo̱ varu̍ṇa̱ mitrārya̍man | avā̱ṁsy ā vṛ̍ṇīmahe ||

**8.67.5**

jī̱vān no̍ a̱bhi dhe̍ta̱nādi̍tyāsaḥ pu̱rā hathā̍t | kad dha̍ stha havanaśrutaḥ ||

**8.67.6**

yad va̍ḥ śrā̱ntāya̍ sunva̱te varū̍tha̱m asti̱ yac cha̱rdiḥ | tenā̍ no̱ adhi̍ vocata ||

**8.67.7**

asti̍ devā a̱ṁhor u̱rv asti̱ ratna̱m anā̍gasaḥ | ādi̍tyā̱ adbhu̍tainasaḥ ||

**8.67.8**

mā na̱ḥ setu̍ḥ siṣed a̱yam ma̱he vṛ̍ṇaktu na̱s pari̍ | indra̱ id dhi śru̱to va̱śī ||

**8.67.9**

mā no̍ mṛ̱cā ri̍pū̱ṇāṁ vṛ̍ji̱nānā̍m aviṣyavaḥ | devā̍ a̱bhi pra mṛ̍kṣata ||

**8.67.10**

u̱ta tvām a̍dite mahy a̱haṁ de̱vy upa̍ bruve | su̱mṛ̱ḻī̱kām a̱bhiṣṭa̍ye ||

**8.67.11**

parṣi̍ dī̱ne ga̍bhī̱ra ām̐ ugra̍putre̱ jighā̍ṁsataḥ | māki̍s to̱kasya̍ no riṣat ||

**8.67.12**

a̱ne̱ho na̍ uruvraja̱ urū̍ci̱ vi prasa̍rtave | kṛ̱dhi to̱kāya̍ jī̱vase̍ ||

**8.67.13**

ye mū̱rdhāna̍ḥ kṣitī̱nām ada̍bdhāsa̱ḥ svaya̍śasaḥ | vra̱tā rakṣa̍nte a̱druha̍ḥ ||

**8.67.14**

te na̍ ā̱sno vṛkā̍ṇā̱m ādi̍tyāso mu̱moca̍ta | ste̱nam ba̱ddham i̍vādite ||

**8.67.15**

apo̱ ṣu ṇa̍ i̱yaṁ śaru̱r ādi̍tyā̱ apa̍ durma̱tiḥ | a̱smad e̱tv aja̍ghnuṣī ||

**8.67.16**

śaśva̱d dhi va̍ḥ sudānava̱ ādi̍tyā ū̱tibhi̍r va̱yam | pu̱rā nū̱nam bu̍bhu̱jmahe̍ ||

**8.67.17**

śaśva̍nta̱ṁ hi pra̍cetasaḥ prati̱yanta̍ṁ ci̱d ena̍saḥ | devā̍ḥ kṛṇu̱tha jī̱vase̍ ||

**8.67.18**

tat su no̱ navya̱ṁ sanya̍sa̱ ādi̍tyā̱ yan mumo̍cati | ba̱ndhād ba̱ddham i̍vādite ||

**8.67.19**

nāsmāka̍m asti̱ tat tara̱ ādi̍tyāso ati̱ṣkade̍ | yū̱yam a̱smabhya̍m mṛḻata ||

**8.67.20**

mā no̍ he̱tir vi̱vasva̍ta̱ ādi̍tyāḥ kṛ̱trimā̱ śaru̍ḥ | pu̱rā nu ja̱raso̍ vadhīt ||

**8.67.21**

vi ṣu dveṣo̱ vy a̍ṁha̱tim ādi̍tyāso̱ vi saṁhi̍tam | viṣva̱g vi vṛ̍hatā̱ rapa̍ḥ ||

**8.68.1**

ā tvā̱ ratha̱ṁ yatho̱taye̍ su̱mnāya̍ vartayāmasi | tu̱vi̱kū̱rmim ṛ̍tī̱ṣaha̱m indra̱ śavi̍ṣṭha̱ satpa̍te ||

**8.68.2**

tuvi̍śuṣma̱ tuvi̍krato̱ śacī̍vo̱ viśva̍yā mate | ā pa̍prātha mahitva̱nā ||

**8.68.3**

yasya̍ te mahi̱nā ma̱haḥ pari̍ jmā̱yanta̍m ī̱yatu̍ḥ | hastā̱ vajra̍ṁ hira̱ṇyaya̍m ||

**8.68.4**

vi̱śvāna̍rasya va̱s pati̱m anā̍natasya̱ śava̍saḥ | evai̍ś ca carṣaṇī̱nām ū̱tī hu̍ve̱ rathā̍nām ||

**8.68.5**

a̱bhiṣṭa̍ye sa̱dāvṛ̍dha̱ṁ sva̍rmīḻheṣu̱ yaṁ nara̍ḥ | nānā̱ hava̍nta ū̱taye̍ ||

**8.68.6**

pa̱romā̱tra̱m ṛcī̍ṣama̱m indra̱m u̱graṁ su̱rādha̍sam | īśā̍naṁ ci̱d vasū̍nām ||

**8.68.7**

taṁ-ta̱m id rādha̍se ma̱ha indra̍ṁ codāmi pī̱taye̍ | yaḥ pū̱rvyām anu̍ṣṭuti̱m īśe̍ kṛṣṭī̱nāṁ nṛ̱tuḥ ||

**8.68.8**

na yasya̍ te śavasāna sa̱khyam ā̱naṁśa̱ martya̍ḥ | naki̱ḥ śavā̍ṁsi te naśat ||

**8.68.9**

tvotā̍sa̱s tvā yu̱jāpsu sūrye̍ ma̱had dhana̍m | jaye̍ma pṛ̱tsu va̍jrivaḥ ||

**8.68.10**

taṁ tvā̍ ya̱jñebhi̍r īmahe̱ taṁ gī̱rbhir gi̍rvaṇastama | indra̱ yathā̍ ci̱d āvi̍tha̱ vāje̍ṣu puru̱māyya̍m ||

**8.68.11**

yasya̍ te svā̱du sa̱khyaṁ svā̱dvī praṇī̍tir adrivaḥ | ya̱jño vi̍tanta̱sāyya̍ḥ ||

**8.68.12**

u̱ru ṇa̍s ta̱nve̱3̱̍ tana̍ u̱ru kṣayā̍ya nas kṛdhi | u̱ru ṇo̍ yandhi jī̱vase̍ ||

**8.68.13**

u̱ruṁ nṛbhya̍ u̱ruṁ gava̍ u̱ruṁ rathā̍ya̱ panthā̍m | de̱vavī̍tim manāmahe ||

**8.68.14**

upa̍ mā̱ ṣaḍ dvā-dvā̱ nara̱ḥ soma̍sya̱ harṣyā̍ | tiṣṭha̍nti svādurā̱taya̍ḥ ||

**8.68.15**

ṛ̱jrāv i̍ndro̱ta ā da̍de̱ harī̱ ṛkṣa̍sya sū̱navi̍ | ā̱śva̱me̱dhasya̱ rohi̍tā ||

**8.68.16**

su̱rathā̍m̐ ātithi̱gve sva̍bhī̱śūm̐r ā̱rkṣe | ā̱śva̱me̱dhe su̱peśa̍saḥ ||

**8.68.17**

ṣaḻ aśvā̍m̐ ātithi̱gva i̍ndro̱te va̱dhūma̍taḥ | sacā̍ pū̱takra̍tau sanam ||

**8.68.18**

aiṣu̍ ceta̱d vṛṣa̍ṇvaty a̱ntar ṛ̱jreṣv aru̍ṣī | sva̱bhī̱śuḥ kaśā̍vatī ||

**8.68.19**

na yu̱ṣme vā̍jabandhavo nini̱tsuś ca̱na martya̍ḥ | a̱va̱dyam adhi̍ dīdharat ||

**8.69.1**

pra-pra̍ vas tri̱ṣṭubha̱m iṣa̍m ma̱ndadvī̍rā̱yenda̍ve | dhi̱yā vo̍ me̱dhasā̍taye̱ pura̱ṁdhyā vi̍vāsati ||

**8.69.2**

na̱daṁ va̱ oda̍tīnāṁ na̱daṁ yoyu̍vatīnām | pati̍ṁ vo̱ aghnyā̍nāṁ dhenū̱nām i̍ṣudhyasi ||

**8.69.3**

tā a̍sya̱ sūda̍dohasa̱ḥ soma̍ṁ śrīṇanti̱ pṛśna̍yaḥ | janma̍n de̱vānā̱ṁ viśa̍s tri̱ṣv ā ro̍ca̱ne di̱vaḥ ||

**8.69.4**

a̱bhi pra gopa̍tiṁ gi̱rendra̍m arca̱ yathā̍ vi̱de | sū̱nuṁ sa̱tyasya̱ satpa̍tim ||

**8.69.5**

ā hara̍yaḥ sasṛjri̱re 'ru̍ṣī̱r adhi̍ ba̱rhiṣi̍ | yatrā̱bhi sa̱ṁnavā̍mahe ||

**8.69.6**

indrā̍ya̱ gāva̍ ā̱śira̍ṁ dudu̱hre va̱jriṇe̱ madhu̍ | yat sī̍m upahva̱re vi̱dat ||

**8.69.7**

ud yad bra̱dhnasya̍ vi̱ṣṭapa̍ṁ gṛ̱ham indra̍ś ca̱ ganva̍hi | madhva̍ḥ pī̱tvā sa̍cevahi̱ triḥ sa̱pta sakhyu̍ḥ pa̱de ||

**8.69.8**

arca̍ta̱ prārca̍ta̱ priya̍medhāso̱ arca̍ta | arca̍ntu putra̱kā u̱ta pura̱ṁ na dhṛ̱ṣṇv a̍rcata ||

**8.69.9**

ava̍ svarāti̱ garga̍ro go̱dhā pari̍ saniṣvaṇat | piṅgā̱ pari̍ caniṣkada̱d indrā̍ya̱ brahmodya̍tam ||

**8.69.10**

ā yat pata̍nty e̱nya̍ḥ su̱dughā̱ ana̍pasphuraḥ | a̱pa̱sphura̍ṁ gṛbhāyata̱ soma̱m indrā̍ya̱ pāta̍ve ||

**8.69.11**

apā̱d indro̱ apā̍d a̱gnir viśve̍ de̱vā a̍matsata | varu̍ṇa̱ id i̱ha kṣa̍ya̱t tam āpo̍ a̱bhy a̍nūṣata va̱tsaṁ sa̱ṁśiśva̍rīr iva ||

**8.69.12**

su̱de̱vo a̍si varuṇa̱ yasya̍ te sa̱pta sindha̍vaḥ | a̱nu̱kṣara̍nti kā̱kuda̍ṁ sū̱rmya̍ṁ suṣi̱rām i̍va ||

**8.69.13**

yo vyatī̱m̐r aphā̍ṇaya̱t suyu̍ktā̱m̐ upa̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ | ta̱kvo ne̱tā tad id vapu̍r upa̱mā yo amu̍cyata ||

**8.69.14**

atīd u̍ śa̱kra o̍hata̱ indro̱ viśvā̱ ati̱ dviṣa̍ḥ | bhi̱nat ka̱nīna̍ oda̱nam pa̱cyamā̍nam pa̱ro gi̱rā ||

**8.69.15**

a̱rbha̱ko na ku̍māra̱ko 'dhi̍ tiṣṭha̱n nava̱ṁ ratha̍m | sa pa̍kṣan mahi̱ṣam mṛ̱gam pi̱tre mā̱tre vi̍bhu̱kratu̍m ||

**8.69.16**

ā tū su̍śipra dampate̱ ratha̍ṁ tiṣṭhā hira̱ṇyaya̍m | adha̍ dyu̱kṣaṁ sa̍cevahi sa̱hasra̍pādam aru̱ṣaṁ sva̍sti̱gām a̍ne̱hasa̍m ||

**8.69.17**

taṁ ghe̍m i̱tthā na̍ma̱svina̱ upa̍ sva̱rāja̍m āsate | artha̍ṁ cid asya̱ sudhi̍ta̱ṁ yad eta̍va āva̱rtaya̍nti dā̱vane̍ ||

**8.69.18**

anu̍ pra̱tnasyauka̍saḥ pri̱yame̍dhāsa eṣām | pūrvā̱m anu̱ praya̍tiṁ vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣo hi̱tapra̍yasa āśata ||

**8.70.1**

yo rājā̍ carṣaṇī̱nāṁ yātā̱ rathe̍bhi̱r adhri̍guḥ | viśvā̍sāṁ taru̱tā pṛta̍nānā̱ṁ jyeṣṭho̱ yo vṛ̍tra̱hā gṛ̱ṇe ||

**8.70.2**

indra̱ṁ taṁ śu̍mbha puruhanma̱nn ava̍se̱ yasya̍ dvi̱tā vi̍dha̱rtari̍ | hastā̍ya̱ vajra̱ḥ prati̍ dhāyi darśa̱to ma̱ho di̱ve na sūrya̍ḥ ||

**8.70.3**

naki̱ṣ ṭaṁ karma̍ṇā naśa̱d yaś ca̱kāra̍ sa̱dāvṛ̍dham | indra̱ṁ na ya̱jñair vi̱śvagū̍rta̱m ṛbhva̍sa̱m adhṛ̍ṣṭaṁ dhṛ̱ṣṇvo̍jasam ||

**8.70.4**

aṣā̍ḻham u̱gram pṛta̍nāsu sāsa̱hiṁ yasmi̍n ma̱hīr u̍ru̱jraya̍ḥ | saṁ dhe̱navo̱ jāya̍māne anonavu̱r dyāva̱ḥ kṣāmo̍ anonavuḥ ||

**8.70.5**

yad dyāva̍ indra te śa̱taṁ śa̱tam bhūmī̍r u̱ta syuḥ | na tvā̍ vajrin sa̱hasra̱ṁ sūryā̱ anu̱ na jā̱tam a̍ṣṭa̱ roda̍sī ||

**8.70.6**

ā pa̍prātha mahi̱nā vṛṣṇyā̍ vṛṣa̱n viśvā̍ śaviṣṭha̱ śava̍sā | a̱smām̐ a̍va maghava̱n goma̍ti vra̱je vajri̍ñ ci̱trābhi̍r ū̱tibhi̍ḥ ||

**8.70.7**

na sī̱m ade̍va āpa̱d iṣa̍ṁ dīrghāyo̱ martya̍ḥ | eta̍gvā ci̱d ya eta̍śā yu̱yoja̍te̱ harī̱ indro̍ yu̱yoja̍te ||

**8.70.8**

taṁ vo̍ ma̱ho ma̱hāyya̱m indra̍ṁ dā̱nāya̍ sa̱kṣaṇi̍m | yo gā̱dheṣu̱ ya āra̍ṇeṣu̱ havyo̱ vāje̱ṣv asti̱ havya̍ḥ ||

**8.70.9**

ud ū̱ ṣu ṇo̍ vaso ma̱he mṛ̱śasva̍ śūra̱ rādha̍se | ud ū̱ ṣu ma̱hyai ma̍ghavan ma̱ghatta̍ya̱ ud i̍ndra̱ śrave̍ se ma̱he ||

**8.70.10**

tvaṁ na̍ indra ṛta̱yus tvā̱nido̱ ni tṛ̍mpasi | madhye̍ vasiṣva tuvinṛmṇo̱rvor ni dā̱saṁ śi̍śnatho̱ hathai̍ḥ ||

**8.70.11**

a̱nyavra̍ta̱m amā̍nuṣa̱m aya̍jvāna̱m ade̍vayum | ava̱ svaḥ sakhā̍ dudhuvīta̱ parva̍taḥ su̱ghnāya̱ dasyu̱m parva̍taḥ ||

**8.70.12**

tvaṁ na̍ indrāsā̱ṁ haste̍ śaviṣṭha dā̱vane̍ | dhā̱nānā̱ṁ na saṁ gṛ̍bhāyāsma̱yur dviḥ saṁ gṛ̍bhāyāsma̱yuḥ ||

**8.70.13**

sakhā̍ya̱ḥ kratu̍m icchata ka̱thā rā̍dhāma śa̱rasya̍ | upa̍stutim bho̱jaḥ sū̱rir yo ahra̍yaḥ ||

**8.70.14**

bhūri̍bhiḥ samaha̱ ṛṣi̍bhir ba̱rhiṣma̍dbhiḥ staviṣyase | yad i̱ttham eka̍m-eka̱m ic chara̍ va̱tsān pa̍rā̱dada̍ḥ ||

**8.70.15**

ka̱rṇa̱gṛhyā̍ ma̱ghavā̍ śaurade̱vyo va̱tsaṁ na̍s tri̱bhya āna̍yat | a̱jāṁ sū̱rir na dhāta̍ve ||

**8.71.1**

tvaṁ no agne mahobhiḥ pāhi viśvasyā arāteḥ | uta dviṣo martyasya ||

**8.71.2**

nahi manyuḥ pauruṣeya īśe hi vaḥ priyajāta | tvam id asi kṣapāvān ||

**8.71.3**

sa no viśvebhir devebhir ūrjo napād bhadraśoce | rayiṁ dehi viśvavāram ||

**8.71.4**

na tam agne arātayo martaṁ yuvanta rāyaḥ | yaṁ trāyase dāśvāṁsam ||

**8.71.5**

yaṁ tvaṁ vipra medhasātāv agne hinoṣi dhanāya | sa tavotī goṣu gantā ||

**8.71.6**

tvaṁ rayim puruvīram agne dāśuṣe martāya | pra ṇo naya vasyo accha ||

**8.71.7**

uruṣyā ṇo mā parā dā aghāyate jātavedaḥ | durādhye3 martāya ||

**8.71.8**

agne mākiṣ ṭe devasya rātim adevo yuyota | tvam īśiṣe vasūnām ||

**8.71.9**

sa no vasva upa māsy ūrjo napān māhinasya | sakhe vaso jaritṛbhyaḥ ||

**8.71.10**

acchā naḥ śīraśociṣaṁ giro yantu darśatam | acchā yajñāso namasā purūvasum purupraśastam ūtaye ||

**8.71.11**

agniṁ sūnuṁ sahaso jātavedasaṁ dānāya vāryāṇām | dvitā yo bhūd amṛto martyeṣv ā hotā mandratamo viśi ||

**8.71.12**

agniṁ vo devayajyayāgnim prayaty adhvare | agniṁ dhīṣu prathamam agnim arvaty agniṁ kṣaitrāya sādhase ||

**8.71.13**

agnir iṣāṁ sakhye dadātu na īśe yo vāryāṇām | agniṁ toke tanaye śaśvad īmahe vasuṁ santaṁ tanūpām ||

**8.71.14**

agnim īḻiṣvāvase gāthābhiḥ śīraśociṣam | agniṁ rāye purumīḻha śrutaṁ naro 'gniṁ sudītaye chardiḥ ||

**8.71.15**

agniṁ dveṣo yotavai no gṛṇīmasy agniṁ śaṁ yoś ca dātave | viśvāsu vikṣv aviteva havyo bhuvad vastur ṛṣūṇām ||

**8.72.1**

haviṣ kṛṇudhvam ā gamad adhvaryur vanate punaḥ | vidvām̐ asya praśāsanam ||

**8.72.2**

ni tigmam abhy a1ṁśuṁ sīdad dhotā manāv adhi | juṣāṇo asya sakhyam ||

**8.72.3**

antar icchanti taṁ jane rudram paro manīṣayā | gṛbhṇanti jihvayā sasam ||

**8.72.4**

jāmy atītape dhanuḥ vayodhā aruhad vanam | dṛṣadaṁ jihvayāvadhīt ||

**8.72.5**

caran vatso ruśann iha nidātāraṁ na vindate | veti stotava ambyam ||

**8.72.6**

uto nv asya yan mahad aśvāvad yojanam bṛhad | dāmā rathasya dadṛśe ||

**8.72.7**

duhanti saptaikām upa dvā pañca sṛjataḥ | tīrthe sindhor adhi svare ||

**8.72.8**

ā daśabhir vivasvata indraḥ kośam acucyavīt | khedayā trivṛtā divaḥ ||

**8.72.9**

pari tridhātur adhvaraṁ jūrṇir eti navīyasī | madhvā hotāro añjate ||

**8.72.10**

siñcanti namasāvatam uccācakram parijmānam | nīcīnabāram akṣitam ||

**8.72.11**

abhyāram id adrayo niṣiktam puṣkare madhu | avatasya visarjane ||

**8.72.12**

gāva upāvatāvatam mahī yajñasya rapsudā | ubhā karṇā hiraṇyayā ||

**8.72.13**

ā sute siñcata śriyaṁ rodasyor abhiśriyam | rasā dadhīta vṛṣabham ||

**8.72.14**

te jānata svam okya1ṁ saṁ vatsāso na mātṛbhiḥ | mitho nasanta jāmibhiḥ ||

**8.72.15**

upa srakveṣu bapsataḥ kṛṇvate dharuṇaṁ divi | indre agnā namaḥ svaḥ ||

**8.72.16**

adhukṣat pipyuṣīm iṣam ūrjaṁ saptapadīm ariḥ | sūryasya sapta raśmibhiḥ ||

**8.72.17**

somasya mitrāvaruṇoditā sūra ā dade | tad āturasya bheṣajam ||

**8.72.18**

uto nv asya yat padaṁ haryatasya nidhānyam | pari dyāṁ jihvayāatanat ||

**8.73.1**

ud īrāthām ṛtāyate yuñjāthām aśvinā ratham | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.2**

nimiṣaś cij javīyasā rathenā yātam aśvinā | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.3**

upa stṛṇītam atraye himena gharmam aśvinā | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.4**

kuha sthaḥ kuha jagmathuḥ kuha śyeneva petathuḥ | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.5**

yad adya karhi karhi cic chuśrūyātam imaṁ havam | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.6**

aśvinā yāmahūtamā nediṣṭhaṁ yāmy āpyam | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.7**

avantam atraye gṛhaṁ kṛṇutaṁ yuvam aśvinā | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.8**

varethe agnim ātapo vadate valgv atraye | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.9**

pra saptavadhrir āśasā dhārām agner aśāyata | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.10**

ihā gataṁ vṛṣaṇvasū śṛṇutam ma imaṁ havam | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.11**

kim idaṁ vām purāṇavaj jarator iva śasyate | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.12**

samānaṁ vāṁ sajātyaṁ samāno bandhur aśvinā | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.13**

yo vāṁ rajāṁsy aśvinā ratho viyāti rodasī | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.14**

ā no gavyebhir aśvyaiḥ sahasrair upa gacchatam | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.15**

mā no gavyebhir aśvyaiḥ sahasrebhir ati khyatam | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.16**

aruṇapsur uṣā abhūd akar jyotir ṛtāvarī | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.17**

aśvinā su vicākaśad vṛkṣam paraśumām̐ iva | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.73.18**

puraṁ na dhṛṣṇav ā ruja kṛṣṇayā bādhito viśā | anti ṣad bhūtu vām avaḥ ||

**8.74.1**

viśo-viśo vo atithiṁ vājayantaḥ purupriyam | agniṁ vo duryaṁ vacaḥ stuṣe śūṣasya manmabhiḥ ||

**8.74.2**

yaṁ janāso haviṣmanto mitraṁ na sarpirāsutim | praśaṁsanti praśastibhiḥ ||

**8.74.3**

panyāṁsaṁ jātavedasaṁ yo devatāty udyatā | havyāny airayad divi ||

**8.74.4**

āganma vṛtrahantamaṁ jyeṣṭham agnim ānavam | yasya śrutarvā bṛhann ārkṣo anīka edhate ||

**8.74.5**

amṛtaṁ jātavedasaṁ tiras tamāṁsi darśatam | ghṛtāhavanam īḍyam ||

**8.74.6**

sabādho yaṁ janā ime3 'gniṁ havyebhir īḻate | juhvānāso yatasrucaḥ ||

**8.74.7**

iyaṁ te navyasī matir agne adhāyy asmad ā | mandra sujāta sukrato 'mūra dasmātithe ||

**8.74.8**

sā te agne śaṁtamā caniṣṭhā bhavatu priyā | tayā vardhasva suṣṭutaḥ ||

**8.74.9**

sā dyumnair dyumninī bṛhad upopa śravasi śravaḥ | dadhīta vṛtratūrye ||

**8.74.10**

aśvam id gāṁ rathaprāṁ tveṣam indraṁ na satpatim | yasya śravāṁsi tūrvatha panyam-panyaṁ ca kṛṣṭayaḥ ||

**8.74.11**

yaṁ tvā gopavano girā caniṣṭhad agne aṅgiraḥ | sa pāvaka śrudhī havam ||

**8.74.12**

yaṁ tvā janāsa īḻate sabādho vājasātaye | sa bodhi vṛtratūrye ||

**8.74.13**

ahaṁ huvāna ārkṣe śrutarvaṇi madacyuti | śardhāṁsīva stukāvināṁ mṛkṣā śīrṣā caturṇām ||

**8.74.14**

māṁ catvāra āśavaḥ śaviṣṭhasya dravitnavaḥ | surathāso abhi prayo vakṣan vayo na tugriyam ||

**8.74.15**

satyam it tvā mahenadi paruṣṇy ava dediśam | nem āpo aśvadātaraḥ śaviṣṭhād asti martyaḥ ||

**8.75.1**

yukṣvā hi devahūtamām̐ aśvām̐ agne rathīr iva | ni hotā pūrvyaḥ sadaḥ ||

**8.75.2**

uta no deva devām̐ acchā voco viduṣṭaraḥ | śrad viśvā vāryā kṛdhi ||

**8.75.3**

tvaṁ ha yad yaviṣṭhya sahasaḥ sūnav āhuta | ṛtāvā yajñiyo bhuvaḥ ||

**8.75.4**

ayam agniḥ sahasriṇo vājasya śatinas patiḥ | mūrdhā kavī rayīṇām ||

**8.75.5**

taṁ nemim ṛbhavo yathā namasva sahūtibhiḥ | nedīyo yajñam aṅgiraḥ ||

**8.75.6**

tasmai nūnam abhidyave vācā virūpa nityayā | vṛṣṇe codasva suṣṭutim ||

**8.75.7**

kam u ṣvid asya senayāgner apākacakṣasaḥ | paṇiṁ goṣu starāmahe ||

**8.75.8**

mā no devānāṁ viśaḥ prasnātīr ivosrāḥ | kṛśaṁ na hāsur aghnyāḥ ||

**8.75.9**

mā naḥ samasya dūḍhya1ḥ paridveṣaso aṁhatiḥ | ūrmir na nāvam ā vadhīt ||

**8.75.10**

namas te agna ojase gṛṇanti deva kṛṣṭayaḥ | amair amitram ardaya ||

**8.75.11**

kuvit su no gaviṣṭaye 'gne saṁveṣiṣo rayim | urukṛd uru ṇas kṛdhi ||

**8.75.12**

mā no asmin mahādhane parā varg bhārabhṛd yathā | saṁvargaṁ saṁ rayiṁ jaya ||

**8.75.13**

anyam asmad bhiyā iyam agne siṣaktu ducchunā | vardhā no amavac chavaḥ ||

**8.75.14**

yasyājuṣan namasvinaḥ śamīm adurmakhasya vā | taṁ ghed agnir vṛdhāvati ||

**8.75.15**

parasyā adhi saṁvato 'varām̐ abhy ā tara | yatrāham asmi tām̐ ava ||

**8.75.16**

vidmā hi te purā vayam agne pitur yathāvasaḥ | adhā te sumnam īmahe ||

**8.76.1**

imaṁ nu māyinaṁ huva indram īśānam ojasā | marutvantaṁ na vṛñjase ||

**8.76.2**

ayam indro marutsakhā vi vṛtrasyābhinac chiraḥ | vajreṇa śataparvaṇā ||

**8.76.3**

vāvṛdhāno marutsakhendro vi vṛtram airayat | sṛjan samudriyā apaḥ ||

**8.76.4**

ayaṁ ha yena vā idaṁ svar marutvatā jitam | indreṇa somapītaye ||

**8.76.5**

marutvantam ṛjīṣiṇam ojasvantaṁ virapśinam | indraṁ gīrbhir havāmahe ||

**8.76.6**

indram pratnena manmanā marutvantaṁ havāmahe | asya somasya pītaye ||

**8.76.7**

marutvām̐ indra mīḍhvaḥ pibā somaṁ śatakrato | asmin yajñe puruṣṭuta ||

**8.76.8**

tubhyed indra marutvate sutāḥ somāso adrivaḥ | hṛdā hūyanta ukthinaḥ ||

**8.76.9**

pibed indra marutsakhā sutaṁ somaṁ diviṣṭiṣu | vajraṁ śiśāna ojasā ||

**8.76.10**

uttiṣṭhann ojasā saha pītvī śipre avepayaḥ | somam indra camū sutam ||

**8.76.11**

anu tvā rodasī ubhe krakṣamāṇam akṛpetām | indra yad dasyuhābhavaḥ ||

**8.76.12**

vācam aṣṭāpadīm ahaṁ navasraktim ṛtaspṛśam | indrāt pari tanvam mame ||

**8.77.1**

jajñāno nu śatakratur vi pṛcchad iti mātaram | ka ugrāḥ ke ha śṛṇvire ||

**8.77.2**

ād īṁ śavasy abravīd aurṇavābham ahīśuvam | te putra santu niṣṭuraḥ ||

**8.77.3**

sam it tān vṛtrahākhidat khe arām̐ iva khedayā | pravṛddho dasyuhābhavat ||

**8.77.4**

ekayā pratidhāpibat sākaṁ sarāṁsi triṁśatam | indraḥ somasya kāṇukā ||

**8.77.5**

abhi gandharvam atṛṇad abudhneṣu rajaḥsv ā | indro brahmabhya id vṛhe ||

**8.77.6**

nir āvidhyad giribhya ā dhārayat pakvam odanam | indro bundaṁ svātatam ||

**8.77.7**

śatabradhna iṣus tava sahasraparṇa eka it | yam indra cakṛṣe yujam ||

**8.77.8**

tena stotṛbhya ā bhara nṛbhyo nāribhyo attave | sadyo jāta ṛbhuṣṭhira ||

**8.77.9**

etā cyautnāni te kṛtā varṣiṣṭhāni parīṇasā | hṛdā vīḍv adhārayaḥ ||

**8.77.10**

viśvet tā viṣṇur ābharad urukramas tveṣitaḥ | śatam mahiṣān kṣīrapākam odanaṁ varāham indra emuṣam ||

**8.77.11**

tuvikṣaṁ te sukṛtaṁ sūmayaṁ dhanuḥ sādhur bundo hiraṇyayaḥ | ubhā te bāhū raṇyā susaṁskṛta ṛdūpe cid ṛdūvṛdhā ||

**8.78.1**

puroḻāśaṁ no andhasa indra sahasram ā bhara | śatā ca śūra gonām ||

**8.78.2**

ā no bhara vyañjanaṁ gām aśvam abhyañjanam | sacā manā hiraṇyayā ||

**8.78.3**

uta naḥ karṇaśobhanā purūṇi dhṛṣṇav ā bhara | tvaṁ hi śṛṇviṣe vaso ||

**8.78.4**

nakīṁ vṛdhīka indra te na suṣā na sudā uta | nānyas tvac chūra vāghataḥ ||

**8.78.5**

nakīm indro nikartave na śakraḥ pariśaktave | viśvaṁ śṛṇoti paśyati ||

**8.78.6**

sa manyum martyānām adabdho ni cikīṣate | purā nidaś cikīṣate ||

**8.78.7**

kratva it pūrṇam udaraṁ turasyāsti vidhataḥ | vṛtraghnaḥ somapāvnaḥ ||

**8.78.8**

tve vasūni saṁgatā viśvā ca soma saubhagā | sudātv aparihvṛtā ||

**8.78.9**

tvām id yavayur mama kāmo gavyur hiraṇyayuḥ | tvām aśvayur eṣate ||

**8.78.10**

taved indrāham āśasā haste dātraṁ canā dade | dinasya vā maghavan sambhṛtasya vā pūrdhi yavasya kāśinā ||

**8.79.1**

ayaṁ kṛtnur agṛbhīto viśvajid udbhid it somaḥ | ṛṣir vipraḥ kāvyena ||

**8.79.2**

abhy ūrṇoti yan nagnam bhiṣakti viśvaṁ yat turam | prem andhaḥ khyan niḥ śroṇo bhūt ||

**8.79.3**

tvaṁ soma tanūkṛdbhyo dveṣobhyo 'nyakṛtebhyaḥ | uru yantāsi varūtham ||

**8.79.4**

tvaṁ cittī tava dakṣair diva ā pṛthivyā ṛjīṣin | yāvīr aghasya cid dveṣaḥ ||

**8.79.5**

arthino yanti ced arthaṁ gacchān id daduṣo rātim | vavṛjyus tṛṣyataḥ kāmam ||

**8.79.6**

vidad yat pūrvyaṁ naṣṭam ud īm ṛtāyum īrayat | prem āyus tārīd atīrṇam ||

**8.79.7**

suśevo no mṛḻayākur adṛptakratur avātaḥ | bhavā naḥ soma śaṁ hṛde ||

**8.79.8**

mā naḥ soma saṁ vīvijo mā vi bībhiṣathā rājan | mā no hārdi tviṣā vadhīḥ ||

**8.79.9**

ava yat sve sadhasthe devānāṁ durmatīr īkṣe | rājann apa dviṣaḥ sedha mīḍhvo apa sridhaḥ sedha ||

**8.80.1**

nahy a1nyam baḻākaram marḍitāraṁ śatakrato | tvaṁ na indra mṛḻaya ||

**8.80.2**

yo naḥ śaśvat purāvithāmṛdhro vājasātaye | sa tvaṁ na indra mṛḻaya ||

**8.80.3**

kim aṅga radhracodanaḥ sunvānasyāvited asi | kuvit sv indra ṇaḥ śakaḥ ||

**8.80.4**

indra pra ṇo ratham ava paścāc cit santam adrivaḥ | purastād enam me kṛdhi ||

**8.80.5**

hanto nu kim āsase prathamaṁ no rathaṁ kṛdhi | upamaṁ vājayuḥ śravaḥ ||

**8.80.6**

avā no vājayuṁ rathaṁ sukaraṁ te kim it pari | asmān su jigyuṣas kṛdhi ||

**8.80.7**

indra dṛhyasva pūr asi bhadrā ta eti niṣkṛtam | iyaṁ dhīr ṛtviyāvatī ||

**8.80.8**

mā sīm avadya ā bhāg urvī kāṣṭhā hitaṁ dhanam | apāvṛktā aratnayaḥ ||

**8.80.9**

turīyaṁ nāma yajñiyaṁ yadā karas tad uśmasi | ād it patir na ohase ||

**8.80.10**

avīvṛdhad vo amṛtā amandīd ekadyūr devā uta yāś ca devīḥ | tasmā u rādhaḥ kṛṇuta praśastam prātar makṣū dhiyāvasur jagamyāt ||

**8.81.1**

ā tū na indra kṣumantaṁ citraṁ grābhaṁ saṁ gṛbhāya | mahāhastī dakṣiṇena ||

**8.81.2**

vidmā hi tvā tuvikūrmiṁ tuvideṣṇaṁ tuvīmagham | tuvimātram avobhiḥ ||

**8.81.3**

nahi tvā śūra devā na martāso ditsantam | bhīmaṁ na gāṁ vārāyante ||

**8.81.4**

eto nv indraṁ stavāmeśānaṁ vasvaḥ svarājam | na rādhasā mardhiṣan naḥ ||

**8.81.5**

pra stoṣad upa gāsiṣac chrāvat sāma gīyamānam | abhi rādhasā jugurat ||

**8.81.6**

ā no bhara dakṣiṇenābhi savyena pra mṛśa | indra mā no vasor nir bhāk ||

**8.81.7**

upa kramasvā bhara dhṛṣatā dhṛṣṇo janānām | adāśūṣṭarasya vedaḥ ||

**8.81.8**

indra ya u nu te asti vājo viprebhiḥ sanitvāḥ | asmābhiḥ su taṁ sanuhi ||

**8.81.9**

sadyojuvas te vājā asmabhyaṁ viśvaścandrāḥ | vaśaiś ca makṣū jarante ||

**8.82.1**

ā pra drava parāvato 'rvāvataś ca vṛtrahan | madhvaḥ prati prabharmaṇi ||

**8.82.2**

tīvrāḥ somāsa ā gahi sutāso mādayiṣṇavaḥ | pibā dadhṛg yathociṣe ||

**8.82.3**

iṣā mandasvād u te araṁ varāya manyave | bhuvat ta indra śaṁ hṛde ||

**8.82.4**

ā tv aśatrav ā gahi ny ukthāni ca hūyase | upame rocane divaḥ ||

**8.82.5**

tubhyāyam adribhiḥ suto gobhiḥ śrīto madāya kam | pra soma indra hūyate ||

**8.82.6**

indra śrudhi su me havam asme sutasya gomataḥ | vi pītiṁ tṛptim aśnuhi ||

**8.82.7**

ya indra camaseṣv ā somaś camūṣu te sutaḥ | pibed asya tvam īśiṣe ||

**8.82.8**

yo apsu candramā iva somaś camūṣu dadṛśe | pibed asya tvam īśiṣe ||

**8.82.9**

yaṁ te śyenaḥ padābharat tiro rajāṁsy aspṛtam | pibed asya tvam īśiṣe ||

**8.83.1**

devānām id avo mahat tad ā vṛṇīmahe vayam | vṛṣṇām asmabhyam ūtaye ||

**8.83.2**

te naḥ santu yujaḥ sadā varuṇo mitro aryamā | vṛdhāsaś ca pracetasaḥ ||

**8.83.3**

ati no viṣpitā puru naubhir apo na parṣatha | yūyam ṛtasya rathyaḥ ||

**8.83.4**

vāmaṁ no astv aryaman vāmaṁ varuṇa śaṁsyam | vāmaṁ hy āvṛṇīmahe ||

**8.83.5**

vāmasya hi pracetasa īśānāśo riśādasaḥ | nem ādityā aghasya yat ||

**8.83.6**

vayam id vaḥ sudānavaḥ kṣiyanto yānto adhvann ā | devā vṛdhāya hūmahe ||

**8.83.7**

adhi na indraiṣāṁ viṣṇo sajātyānām | itā maruto aśvinā ||

**8.83.8**

pra bhrātṛtvaṁ sudānavo 'dha dvitā samānyā | mātur garbhe bharāmahe ||

**8.83.9**

yūyaṁ hi ṣṭhā sudānava indrajyeṣṭhā abhidyavaḥ || adhā cid va uta bruve ||

**8.84.1**

preṣṭhaṁ vo atithiṁ stuṣe mitram iva priyam | agniṁ rathaṁ na vedyam ||

**8.84.2**

kavim iva pracetasaṁ yaṁ devāso adha dvitā | ni martyeṣv ādaduḥ ||

**8.84.3**

tvaṁ yaviṣṭha dāśuṣo nṝṁḥ pāhi śṛṇudhī giraḥ | rakṣā tokam uta tmanā ||

**8.84.4**

kayā te agne aṅgira ūrjo napād upastutim | varāya deva manyave ||

**8.84.5**

dāśema kasya manasā yajñasya sahaso yaho | kad u voca idaṁ namaḥ ||

**8.84.6**

adhā tvaṁ hi nas karo viśvā asmabhyaṁ sukṣitīḥ | vājadraviṇaso giraḥ ||

**8.84.7**

kasya nūnam parīṇaso dhiyo jinvasi dampate | goṣātā yasya te giraḥ ||

**8.84.8**

tam marjayanta sukratum puroyāvānam ājiṣu | sveṣu kṣayeṣu vājinam ||

**8.84.9**

kṣeti kṣemebhiḥ sādhubhir nakir yaṁ ghnanti hanti yaḥ | agne suvīra edhate ||

**8.85.1**

ā me havaṁ nāsatyāśvinā gachataṁ yuvam | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.2**

imam me stomam aśvinEmam me śṛṇutaṁ havam | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.3**

ayaṁ vāṁ kṛṣṇo aśvinā havate vājinīvasū | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.4**

śṛṇutaṁ jaritur havaṁ kṛṣṇasya stuvato narā | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.5**

chardir yantam adābhyaṁ viprāya stuvate narā | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.6**

gachataṁ dāśuṣo gṛham itthā stuvato aśvinā | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.7**

yuñjāthāṁ rāsabhaṁ rathe vīḍvaṅge vṛṣaṇvasū | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.8**

trivandureṇa trivṛtā rathenā yātam aśvinā | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.85.9**

nū me giro nāsatyāśvinā prāvataṁ yuvam | madhvaḥ somasya pītaye ||

**8.86.1**

ubhā hi dasrā bhiṣajā mayobhuvo ubhā dakṣasya vacaso babhūvathuḥ | tā vāṁ viśvako havate tanūkṛthe mā no vi yauṣṭaṁ sakhyā mumocatam ||

**8.86.2**

kathā nūnaṁ vāṁ vimanā upa stavad yuvaṁ dhiyaṁ dadathur vasyaiṣṭaye | tā vāṁ viśvako havate tanūkṛthe mā no vi yauṣṭaṁ sakhyā mumocatam ||

**8.86.3**

yuvaṁ hi ṣmā purubhuje mam edhatuṁ viṣṇāpve dadathur vasyaiṣṭaye | tā vāṁ viśvako havate tanūkṛthe mā no vi yauṣṭaṁ sakhyā mumocatam ||

**8.86.4**

uta tyaṁ vīraṁ dhanasām ṛjīṣiṇaṁ dūre cit santam avase havāmahe | yasya svādiṣṭhā sumatiḥ pitur yathā mā no vi yauṣṭaṁ sakhyā mumocatam ||

**8.86.5**

ṛtena devaḥ savitā śamāyata ṛtasya śṛṅgam urviyā vi paprathe | ṛtaṁ sāsāha mahi cit pṛtanyato mā no vi yauṣṭaṁ sakhyā mumocatam ||

**8.87.1**

dyumnī vāṁ stomo aśvinā krivir na seka ā gatam | madhvaḥ sutasya sa divi priyo narā pātaṁ gaurāv iveriṇe ||

**8.87.2**

pibataṁ gharmam madhumantam aśvinā barhiḥ sīdataṁ narā | tā mandasānā manuṣo duroṇa ā ni pātaṁ vedasā vayaḥ ||

**8.87.3**

ā vāṁ viśvābhir ūtibhiḥ priyamedhā ahūṣata | tā vartir yātam upa vṛktabarhiṣo juṣṭaṁ yajñaṁ diviṣṭiṣu ||

**8.87.4**

pibataṁ somam madhumantam aśvinā barhiḥ sīdataṁ sumat | tā vāvṛdhānā upa suṣṭutiṁ divo gantaṁ gaurāv iveriṇam ||

**8.87.5**

ā nūnaṁ yātam aśvināśvebhiḥ pruṣitapsubhiḥ | dasrā hiraṇyavartanī śubhas patī pātaṁ somam ṛtāvṛdhā ||

**8.87.6**

vayaṁ hi vāṁ havāmahe vipanyavo viprāso vājasātaye | tā valgū dasrā purudaṁsasā dhiyāśvinā śruṣṭy ā gatam ||

**8.88.1**

taṁ vo dasmam ṛtīṣahaṁ vasor mandānam andhasaḥ | abhi vatsaṁ na svaseṣu dhenava indraṁ gīrbhir navāmahe ||

**8.88.2**

dyukṣaṁ sudānuṁ taviṣībhir āvṛtaṁ giriṁ na purubhojasam | kṣumantaṁ vājaṁ śatinaṁ sahasriṇam makṣū gomantam īmahe ||

**8.88.3**

na tvā bṛhanto adrayo varanta indra vīḍavaḥ | yad ditsasi stuvate māvate vasu nakiṣ ṭad ā mināti te ||

**8.88.4**

yoddhāsi kratvā śavasota daṁsanā viśvā jātābhi majmanā | ā tvāyam arka ūtaye vavartati yaṁ gotamā ajījanan ||

**8.88.5**

pra hi ririṣa ojasā divo antebhyas pari | na tvā vivyāca raja indra pārthivam anu svadhāṁ vavakṣitha ||

**8.88.6**

nakiḥ pariṣṭir maghavan maghasya te yad dāśuṣe daśasyasi | asmākam bodhy ucathasya codita maṁhiṣṭho vājasātaye ||

**8.89.1**

bṛhad indrāya gāyata maruto vṛtrahaṁtamam | yena jyotir ajanayann ṛtāvṛdho devaṁ devāya jāgṛvi ||

**8.89.2**

apādhamad abhiśastīr aśastihāthendro dyumny ābhavat | devās ta indra sakhyāya yemire bṛhadbhāno marudgaṇa ||

**8.89.3**

pra va indrāya bṛhate maruto brahmārcata | vṛtraṁ hanati vṛtrahā śatakratur vajreṇa śataparvaṇā ||

**8.89.4**

abhi pra bhara dhṛṣatā dhṛṣanmanaḥ śravaś cit te asad bṛhat | arṣantv āpo javasā vi mātaro hano vṛtraṁ jayā svaḥ ||

**8.89.5**

yaj jāyathā apūrvya maghavan vṛtrahatyāya | tat pṛthivīm aprathayaṁs tad astabhnā uta dyām ||

**8.89.6**

tat te yajño ajāyata tad arka uta haskṛtiḥ | tad viśvam abhibhūr asi yaj jātaṁ yac ca jantvam ||

**8.89.7**

āmāsu pakvam airaya ā sūryaṁ rohayo divi | gharmaṁ na sāman tapatā suvṛktibhir juṣṭaṁ girvaṇase bṛhat ||

**8.90.1**

ā no viśvāsu havya indraḥ samatsu bhūṣatu | upa brahmāṇi savanāni vṛtrahā paramajyā ṛcīṣamaḥ ||

**8.90.2**

tvaṁ dātā prathamo rādhasām asy asi satya īśānakṛt | tuvidyumnasya yujyā vṛṇīmahe putrasya śavaso mahaḥ ||

**8.90.3**

brahmā ta indra girvaṇaḥ kriyante anatidbhutā | imā juṣasva haryaśva yojanendra yā te amānmahi ||

**8.90.4**

tvaṁ hi satyo maghavann anānato vṛtrā bhūri nyṛñjase | sa tvaṁ śaviṣṭha vajrahasta dāśuṣe 'rvāñcaṁ rayim ā kṛdhi ||

**8.90.5**

tvam indra yaśā asy ṛjīṣī śavasas pate | tvaṁ vṛtrāṇi haṁsy apratīny eka id anuttā carṣaṇīdhṛtā ||

**8.90.6**

tam u tvā nūnam asura pracetasaṁ rādho bhāgam ivemahe | mahīva kṛttiḥ śaraṇā ta indra pra te sumnā no aśnavan ||

**8.91.1**

kanyā vār avāyatī somam api srutāvidat | astam bharanty abravīd indrāya sunavai tvā śakrāya sunavai tvā ||

**8.91.2**
