asau ya eṣi vīrako gṛhaṁ-gṛhaṁ vicākaśad | imaṁ jambhasutam piba dhānāvantaṁ karambhiṇam apūpavantam ukthinam ||

**8.91.3**

ā cana tvā cikitsāmo 'dhi cana tvā nemasi | śanair iva śanakair ivendrāyendo pari srava ||

**8.91.4**

kuvic chakat kuvit karat kuvinn no vasyasas karat | kuvit patidviṣo yatīr indreṇa saṁgamāmahai ||

**8.91.5**

imāni trīṇi viṣṭapā tānīndra vi rohaya | śiras tatasyo rvarāṁm ād idam ma upodare ||

**8.91.6**

asau ca yā na urvarād imāṁ tanvaṁ mama | atho tatasya yac chiraḥ sarvā tā romaśā kṛdhi ||

**8.91.7**

khe rathasya khe 'nasaḥ khe yugasya śatakrato | apālām indra triṣ pūtvy akṛṇoḥ sūryatvacam ||

**8.92.1**

pāntam ā vo andhasa indram abhi pra gāyata | viśvāsāhaṁ śatakratum maṁhiṣṭhaṁ carṣaṇīnām ||

**8.92.2**

puruhūtam puruṣṭutaṁ gāthānyaṁ sanaśrutam | indra iti bravītana ||

**8.92.3**

indra in no mahānāṁ dātā vājānāṁ nṛtuḥ | mahāṁ abhijñv ā yamat ||

**8.92.4**

apād u śipry andhasaḥ sudakṣasya proṣiṇaḥ | indor indro yavāśiraḥ ||

**8.92.5**

tam v abhi prārcatendraṁ somasya pītaye | tad id dhy asya vardhanam ||

**8.92.6**

asya pītvā madānāṁ devo devasyaujasā | viśvābhi bhuvanā bhuvat ||

**8.92.7**

tyam u vaḥ satrāsāhaṁ viśvāsu gīrṣv āyatam | ā cyāvayasy ūtaye ||

**8.92.8**

yudhmaṁ santam anarvāṇaṁ somapām anapacyutam | naram avāryakratum ||

**8.92.9**

śikṣā ṇa indra rāya ā puru vidvāṁ ṛcīṣama | avā naḥ pārye dhane ||

**8.92.10**

ataś cid indra ṇa upā yāhi śatavājayā | iṣā sahasravājayā ||

**8.92.11**

ayāma dhīvato dhiyo 'rvadbhiḥ śakra godare | jayema pṛtsu vajrivaḥ ||

**8.92.12**

vayam u tvā śatakrato gāvo na yavaseṣv ā | uktheṣu raṇayāmasi ||

**8.92.13**

viśvā hi martyatvanānukāmā śatakrato | aganma vajrinn āśasaḥ ||

**8.92.14**

tve su putra śavasovṛtran kāmakātayaḥ | na tvām indrāti ricyate ||

**8.92.15**

sa no vṛṣan saniṣṭhayā saṁ ghorayā dravitnvā | dhiyāviḍḍhi puraṁdhyā ||

**8.92.16**

yas te nūnaṁ śatakratav indra dyumnitamo madaḥ | tena nūnam made madeḥ ||

**8.92.17**

yas te citraśravastamo ya indra vṛtrahantamaḥ | ya ojodātamo madaḥ ||

**8.92.18**

vidmā hi yas te adrivas tvādattaḥ satya somapāḥ | viśvāsu dasma kṛṣṭiṣu ||

**8.92.19**

indrāya madvane sutam pari ṣṭobhantu no giraḥ | arkam arcantu kāravaḥ ||

**8.92.20**

yasmin viśvā adhi śriyo raṇanti sapta saṁsadaḥ | indraṁ sute havāmahe ||

**8.92.21**

trikadrukeṣu cetanaṁ devāso yajñam atnata | tam id vardhantu no giraḥ ||

**8.92.22**

ā tvā viśantv indavaḥ samudram iva sindhavaḥ | na tvām indrāti ricyate ||

**8.92.23**

vivyaktha mahinā vṛṣan bhakṣaṁ somasya jāgṛve | ya indra jaṭhareṣu te ||

**8.92.24**

araṁ ta indra kukṣaye somo bhavatu vṛtrahan | araṁ dhāmabhya indavaḥ ||

**8.92.25**

aram aśvāya gāyati śrutakakṣo araṁ gave | aram indrasya dhāmne ||

**8.92.26**

araṁ hi ṣma suteṣu naḥ someṣv indra bhūṣasi | araṁ te śakra dāvane ||

**8.92.27**

parākāttāc cid adrivas tvāṁ nakṣanta no giraḥ | araṁ gamāma te vayam ||

**8.92.28**

evā hy asi vīrayur evā śūra uta sthiraḥ | evā te rādhyam manaḥ ||

**8.92.29**

evā rātis tuvīmagha viśvebhir dhāyi dhātṛbhiḥ | adhā cid indra me sacā ||

**8.92.30**

mo ṣu brahmeva tandrayur bhuvo vājānām pate | matsvā sutasya gomataḥ ||

**8.92.31**

mā na indrābhy ādiśaḥ sūro aktuṣv ā yaman | tvā yujā vanema tat ||

**8.92.32**

tvayed indra yujā vayam prati bruvīmahi spṛdhaḥ | tvam asmākaṁ tava smasi ||

**8.92.33**

tvām id dhi tvāyavo anunuvutaś carān | sakhāya indra kāravaḥ ||

**8.93.1**

ud ghed abhi śrutāmaghaṁ vṛṣabhaṁ naryāpasam | astāram eṣi sūrya ||

**8.93.2**

nava yo navatiṁ puro bibheda bāhvojasā | ahiṁ ca vṛtrahāvadhīt ||

**8.93.3**

sa na indraḥ śivaḥ sakhā aśvāvad gomad yavamat | urudhāreva dohate ||

**8.93.4**

yad adya kac ca vṛtrahann udagā abhi sūrya | sarvaṁ tad indra te vaśe ||

**8.93.5**

yad vā pravṛddha satpate na marā iti manyase | uto tat satyam it tava ||

**8.93.6**

ye somāsaḥ parāvati ye arvāvati sunvire | sarvāṁs tāṁ indra gacchasi ||

**8.93.7**

tam indraṁ vājayāmasi mahe vṛtrāya hantave | sa vṛṣā vṛṣabho bhuvat ||

**8.93.8**

indraḥ sa dāmane kṛta ojiṣṭhaḥ sa made hitaḥ | dyumnī ślokī sa somyaḥ ||

**8.93.9**

girā vajro na sambhṛtaḥ sabalo anapacyutaḥ | vavakṣa ṛṣvo astṛtaḥ ||

**8.93.10**

durge cin naḥ sugaṁ kṛdhi gṛṇāna indra girvaṇaḥ | tvaṁ ca maghavan vaśaḥ ||

**8.93.11**

yasya te nū cid ādiśaṁ na minanti svarājyam | na devo nādhrigur janaḥ ||

**8.93.12**

adhā te apratiṣkutaṁ devī śuṣmaṁ saparyataḥ | ubhe suśipra rodasī ||

**8.93.13**

tvam etad adhārayaḥ kṛṣṇāsu rohiṇīṣu ca | paruṣṇīṣu ruśat payaḥ ||

**8.93.14**

vi yad aher adha tviṣo viśve devāso akramuḥ | vidan mṛgasya tām̐ amaḥ ||

**8.93.15**

ād u me nivaro bhuvad vṛtrahādiṣṭa pauṁsyam | ajātaśatrur astṛtaḥ ||

**8.93.16**

śrutaṁ vo vṛtrahantamam pra śardhaṁ carṣaṇīnām | ā śuṣe rādhase mahe ||

**8.93.17**

ayā dhiyā ca gavyayā puruṇāman puruṣṭuta | yat some-soma ābhavaḥ ||

**8.93.18**

bodhinmanā id astu no vṛtrahā bhūryāsutiḥ | śṛṇotu śakra āśiṣam ||

**8.93.19**

kayā tvaṁ na ūtyābhi pra mandase vṛṣan | kayā stotṛbhya ā bhara ||

**8.93.20**

kasya vṛṣā sute sacā niyutvān vṛṣabho raṇat | vṛtrahā somapītaye ||

**8.93.21**

abhī ṣu ṇas tvaṁ rayim mandasānaḥ sahasriṇam | prayantā bodhi dāśuṣe ||

**8.93.22**

patnīvantaḥ sutā ima uśanto yanti vītaye | apāṁ jagmir nicumpuṇaḥ ||

**8.93.23**

iṣṭā hotrā asṛkṣatendraṁ vṛdhāso adhvare | acchāvabhṛtham ojasā ||

**8.93.24**

iha tyā sadhamādyā harī hiraṇyakeśyā | voḻhām abhi prayo hitam ||

**8.93.25**

tubhyaṁ somāḥ sutā ime stīrṇam barhir vibhāvaso | stotṛbhya indram ā vaha ||

**8.93.26**

ā te dakṣaṁ vi rocanā dadhad ratnā vi dāśuṣe | stotṛbhya indram arcata ||

**8.93.27**

ā te dadhāmīndriyam ukthā viśvā śatakrato | stotṛbhya indra mṛḻaya ||

**8.93.28**

bhadram-bhadraṁ na ā bhareṣam ūrjaṁ śatakrato | yad indra mṛḻayāsi naḥ ||

**8.93.29**

sa no viśvāny ā bhara suvitāni śatakrato | yad indra mṛḻayāsi naḥ ||

**8.93.30**

tvām id vṛtrahantama sutāvanto havāmahe | yad indra mṛḻayāsi naḥ ||

**8.93.31**

upa no haribhiḥ sutaṁ yāhi madānām pate | upa no haribhiḥ sutam ||

**8.93.32**

dvitā yo vṛtrahantamo vida indraḥ śatakratuḥ | upa no haribhiḥ sutam ||

**8.93.33**

tvaṁ hi vṛtrahann eṣām pātā somānām asi | upa no haribhiḥ sutam ||

**8.93.34**

indra iṣe dadātu na ṛbhukṣaṇam ṛbhuṁ rayim | vājī dadātu vājinam ||

**8.94.1**

gaur dhayati marutāṁ śravasyur mātā maghonām | yuktā vahnī rathānām ||

**8.94.2**

yasyā devā upasthe vratā viśve dhārayante | sūryāmāsā dṛśe kam ||

**8.94.3**

tat su no viśve arya ā sadā gṛṇanti kāravaḥ | marutaḥ somapītaye ||

**8.94.4**

asti somo ayaṁ sutaḥ pibanty asya marutaḥ | uta svarājo aśvinā ||

**8.94.5**

pibanti mitro aryamā tanā pūtasya varuṇaḥ | triṣadsthasya jāvataḥ ||

**8.94.6**

uto nv asya joṣam āṁ indraḥ sutasya gomataḥ | prātar hoteva matsati ||

**8.94.7**

kad atviṣanta sūrayas tira āpa iva sridhaḥ | arṣanti pūtadakṣasaḥ ||

**8.94.8**

kad vo adya mahānāṁ devānām avo vṛṇe | tmanā ca dasmavarcasām ||

**8.94.9**

ā ye viśvā pārthivāni paprathan rocanā divaḥ | marutaḥ somapītaye ||

**8.94.10**

tyān nu pūtadakṣaso divo vo maruto huve | asya somasya pītaye ||

**8.94.11**

tyān nu ye vi rodasī tastabhur maruto huve | asya somasya pītaye ||

**8.94.12**

tyaṁ nu mārutaṁ gaṇaṁ giriṣṭhāṁ vṛṣaṇaṁ huve | asya somasya pītaye ||

**8.95.1**

ā tvā giro rathīr ivāsthuḥ suteṣu girvaṇaḥ | abhi tvā sam anūṣatendra vatsaṁ na mātaraḥ ||

**8.95.2**

ā tvā śukrā acucyavuḥ sutāsa indra girvaṇaḥ | pibā tv asyāndhasa indra viśvāsu te hitam ||

**8.95.3**

pibā somam madāya kam indra śyenābhṛtaṁ sutam | tvaṁ hi śaśvatīnāṁ patī rājā viśām asi ||

**8.95.4**

śrudhī havaṁ tirascyā indra yas tvā saparyati | suvīryasya gomato rāyas pūrdhi mahāṁ asi ||

**8.95.5**

indra yas te navīyasīṁ giram mandrām ajījanat | cikitvinnmanasaṁ dhiyam pratnām ṛtasya pipyuṣīm ||

**8.95.6**

tam u ṣṭavāma yaṁ gira indram ukthāni vāvṛduḥ | purūṇy asya pauṁsyā siṣāsanto vanāmahe ||

**8.95.7**

eto nv indraṁ stavāma śuddhaṁ śuddhena sāmnā | śuddhair ukthair vāvṛdhvāṁsaṁ śuddha āśīrvān mamattu ||

**8.95.8**

indra śuddho na ā gahi śuddhaḥ śuddhābhir ūtibhiḥ | śuddho rayiṁ ni dhāraya śuddho mamaddhi somyaḥ ||

**8.95.9**

indra śuddho hi no rayiṁ śuddho ratnāni dāśuṣe | śuddho vṛtrāṇi jighnase śuddho vājaṁ siṣāsasi ||

**8.96.1**

asmā uṣāsa ātiranta yāmam indrāya naktam ūrmyāḥ suvācaḥ | asmā āpo mātaraḥ sapta tasthu nṛbhyas tarāya sindhavaḥ supārāḥ ||

**8.96.2**

atividdhā vithureṇā cid astrā triḥ sapta sānu saṁhitā girīṇām | na tad devo na martyas tuturyād yāni pravṛddho vṛṣabhaś cakāra ||

**8.96.3**

indrasya vajra āyaso nimiśla indrasya bāhvor bhūyiṣṭham ojaḥ | śīrṣann indrasya kratavo nireka āsann eṣanta śrutyā upāke ||

**8.96.4**

manye tvā yajñiyaṁ yajñiyānāṁ manye tvā cyavanam acyutānām | manye tvā satvanām indra ketum manye tvā vṛṣabhaṁ carṣaṇīnām ||

**8.96.5**

ā yad vajram bāhvor indra dhatse madacyutam ahaye hantavā u | pra parvatā anavanta pra gāvaḥ pra brahmāṇo abhinakṣanta indram ||

**8.96.6**

tam u ṣṭavāma ya imā jajāna viśvā jātāny avarāṇy asmāt | indreṇa mitraṁ didhiṣema gīrbhir upo namobhir vṛṣabhaṁ viśema ||

**8.96.7**

vṛtrasya tvā śvasathād īṣamāṇā viśve devā ajahur ye sakhāyaḥ | marudbhir indra sakhyaṁ te astv athemā viśvāḥ pṛtanā jayāsi ||

**8.96.8**

triḥ ṣaṣṭis tvā maruto vāvṛdhānā usrā iva rāśayo yajñiyāsaḥ | upa tvemaḥ kṛdhi no bhāgadheya śuṣmaṁ ta enā haviṣā vidhema ||

**8.96.9**

tigmam āyudham marutām anīkaṁ kas ta indra prati vajraṁ dadharṣa | anāyudhāso asurā adevāś cakreṇa tāṁ apa vapa ṛjīṣin ||

**8.96.10**

maha ugrāya tavase suvṛktim preraya śivatamāya paśvaḥ | girvāhase gira indrāya pūrvīr dhehi tanve kuvid aṅga vedat ||

**8.96.11**

ukthavāhase vibhve manīṣāṁ druṇā na pāram īrayā nadīnām | ni spṛśa dhiyā tanvi śrutasya juṣṭatarasya kuvid aṅga vedat ||

**8.96.12**

tad viviḍḍhi yat ta indro jujoṣat stuhi suṣṭutiṁ namasā vivāsa | upa bhūṣa jaritar mā ruvaṇyaḥ śrāvayā vācaṁ kuvid aṅga vedat ||

**8.96.13**

ava drapso aṁśumatīm atiṣṭhad iyānaḥ kṛṣṇo daśabhiḥ sahasraiḥ | āvat tam indraḥ śacyā dhamantam apa snehitīr nṛmaṇā adhatta ||

**8.96.14**

drapsam apaśyaṁ viṣuṇe carantam upahvare nadyo aṁśumatyāḥ | nabho na kṛṣṇam avatasthi āṁsa iṣyāmi vo vṛṣaṇo yudhyatājau ||

**8.96.15**

adha drapso aṁśumatyā upasthe adhārayat tanvaṁ titviṣāṇaḥ | viśo adevīr abhy ācarantīr bṛhaspatinā yujendraḥ sasāhe ||

**8.96.16**

tvaṁ ha tyat saptabhyo jāyamāno aśatrubhyo abhavaḥ śatrur indra | gūḍhe dyāvāpṛthivī anv avindo vibhumadbhyo bhuvanebhyo raṇaṁ dhāḥ ||

**8.96.17**

tvaṁ ha tyad apratimānam ojo vajreṇa vajrin dhṛṣito jaghantha | tvaṁ śuṣṇasyāvātiro vadhatrais tvaṁ gā indra śacyed avindaḥ ||

**8.96.18**

tvaṁ ha tyad vṛṣabha carṣaṇīnāṁ ghano vṛtrāṇāṁ taviṣo babhūtha | tvaṁ sindhūṁr asṛjas tastabhānān tvam apo ajayo dāsapatnīḥ ||

**8.96.19**

sa sukratū raṇitā yaḥ suteṣv anuttamanyu yo aheva revān | ya eka in nary apāṁsi kartā sa vṛtrahā pratīd anyam āhuḥ ||

**8.96.20**

sa vṛtrahendraś carṣaṇīdhṛt taṁ suṣṭutyā havyaṁ huvema | sa prāvitā maghavā no adhivaktā sa vājasya śravasyasya dātā ||

**8.96.21**

sa vṛtrahendra ṛbhukṣāḥ sadyo jajñāno havyo babhūva | kṛṇvann apāṁsi naryā purūṇi somo na pīto havyaḥ sakhibhyaḥ ||

**8.97.1**

yā indra bhuja ābharaḥ svarvāṁ asurebhyaḥ | stotāram in maghavann asya vardhaya ye ca tve vṛktabarhiṣaḥ ||

**8.97.2**

yam indra dadhiṣe tvam aśvaṁ gām bhāgam avyayam | yajamāne sunvati dakṣiṇāvati tasmin taṁ dhehi mā paṇau ||

**8.97.3**

ya indra sasty avrato anuṣvāpam adevayuḥ | svaiḥ ṣa evair mumurat poṣyaṁ rayiṁ sanutar dhehi taṁ tataḥ ||

**8.97.4**

yac chakrāsi parāvati yad arvāvati vṛtrahan | atas tvā gīrbhir dyugad indra keśibhiḥ sutāvāṁ ā vivāsati ||

**8.97.5**

yad vāsi rocane divaḥ samudrasyādhi viṣṭapi | yat pārthive sadane vṛtrahantama yad antarikṣa ā gahi ||

**8.97.6**

sa naḥ someṣu somapāḥ suteṣu śavasas pate | mādayasva rādhasā sūnṛtāvatendra rāyā parīṇasā ||

**8.97.7**

mā na indra parā vṛṇag bhavā naḥ sadhamādyaḥ | tvaṁ na ūtī tvam in na āpyam mā na indra parā vṛṇak ||

**8.97.8**

asme indra sacā sute ni ṣadā pītaye madhu | kṛdhī jaritre maghavann avo mahad asme indra sacā sute ||

**8.97.9**

na tvā devāsa āśata na martyāso adrivaḥ | viśvā jātāni śavasābhibhūr asi na tvā devāsa āśata ||

**8.97.10**

viśvāḥ pṛtanā abhibhūtaraṁ naraṁ sajūs tatakṣur indraṁ jajanś ca rājase | kratvā variṣṭhaṁ vara āmurim utogram ojiṣṭhaṁ tavasaṁ tarasvinam ||

**8.97.11**

sam īṁ rebhāso asvarann indraṁ somasya pītaye | svarpatiṁ yad īṁ vṛdhe dhṛtavrato hy ojasā sam ūtibhiḥ ||

**8.97.12**

nemiṁ namanti cakṣasā meṣaṁ viprā abhisvarā | sudītayo vo adruho api karṇe tarasvinaḥ sam ṛkvabhiḥ ||

**8.97.13**

tam indraṁ johavīmi maghavānam ugraṁ satrā dadhānam apratiṣkutaṁ śavāṁsi | maṁhiṣṭho gīrbhir ā ca yajñiyo vavartad rāye no viśvā supathā kṛṇotu vajrī ||

**8.97.14**

tvam pura indra cikid enā vy ojasā śaviṣṭha śakra nāśayadhyai | tvad viśvāni bhuvanāni vajrin dyāvā rejete pṛthivī ca bhīṣā ||

**8.97.15**

tan ma ṛtam indra śūra citra pātv apo na vajrin duritāti parṣi bhūri | kadā na indra rāya ā daśasyer viśvapsnyasya spṛhayāyyasya rājan ||

**8.98.1**

indrāya sāma gāyata viprāya bṛhate bṛhat | dharmakṛte vipaścite panasyave ||

**8.98.2**

tvam indrābhibhūr asi tvaṁ sūryam arocayaḥ | viśvakarmā viśvadevo mahāṁ asi ||

**8.98.3**

vibhrājañ jyotiṣā svar agaccho rocanaṁ divaḥ | devās ta indra sakhyāya yemire ||

**8.98.4**

endra no gadhi priyaḥ satrājid agohyaḥ | girir na viśvatas pṛthuḥ patir divaḥ ||

**8.98.5**

abhi hi satya somapā ubhe babhūtha rodasī | indrāsi sunvato vṛdhaḥ patir divaḥ ||

**8.98.6**

tvaṁ hi śaśvatīnām indra dartā purām asi | hantā dasyor manor vṛdhaḥ patir divaḥ ||

**8.98.7**

adhā hīndra girvaṇa upa tvā kāmān mahaḥ sasṛjmahe | udeva yanta udabhiḥ ||

**8.98.8**

vār ṇa tvā yavyābhir vardhanti śūra brahmāṇi | vāvṛdhvāṁsaṁ cid adrivo dive-dive ||

**8.98.9**

yuñjanti harī iṣirasya gāthayorau rathe uruyuge | indravāhā vacoyujā ||

**8.98.10**

tvaṁ na indrā bharaṁ ojo nṛmṇaṁ śatakrato vicarṣaṇe | ā vīram pṛtanāṣaham ||

**8.98.11**

tvaṁ hi naḥ pitā vaso tvam mātā śatakrato babhūvitha | adhā te sumnam īmahe ||

**8.98.12**

tvāṁ śuṣmin puruhūta vājayantam upa bruve śatakrato | sa no rāsva suvīryam ||

**8.99.1**

tvām idā hyo naro apīpyan vajrin bhūrṇayaḥ | sa indra stomavāhasām iha śrudhy upa svasaram ā gahi ||

**8.99.2**

matsvā suśipra harivas tad īmahe tve ā bhūṣanti vedhasaḥ | tava śravāṁsy upamāny ukthyā suteṣv indra girvaṇaḥ ||

**8.99.3**

śrāyanta iva sūryaṁ viśved indrasya bhakṣata | vasūni jāte janmāna ojasā prati bhāgaṁ na dīdhima ||

**8.99.4**

anarśarātiṁ vasudām upa stuhi bhadrā indrasya rātayaḥ | so asya kāmaṁ vidhato na roṣati mano dānāya codayan ||

**8.99.5**

tvam indra pratūrtiṣv abhi viśvā asi spṛdhaḥ | aśastihā janitā viśvatūr asi tvaṁ tūrya taruṣyataḥ ||

**8.99.6**

anu te śuṣmaṁ turayantam īyatuḥ kṣoṇī śiśuṁ na mātarā | viśvās te spṛdhaḥ śnathayanta manyave vṛtraṁ yad indra tūrvasi ||

**8.99.7**

ita ūtī vo ajaram praheatāram aprahitam | āśuṁ jetāraṁ hetāraṁ rathītamam atūrtaṁ tugryāvṛdham ||

**8.99.8**

iṣkartāram aniṣkṛtaṁ sahaskṛtaṁ śatamūtiṁ śatakratum | samānam indram avase havāmahe vasavānaṁ vasūjuvam ||

**8.100.1**

ayaṁ ta emi tanvā purastād viśve devā abhi mā yanti paścāt | yadā mahyaṁ dīdharo bhāgam indrād in mayā kṛṇavo vīryāṇi ||

**8.100.2**

dadhāmi te madhuno bhakṣam agre hitas te bhāgaḥ suto astu somaḥ | asaś ca tvaṁ dakṣiṇataḥ sakhā me adhā vṛtrāṇi jaṅghanāva bhūri ||

**8.100.3**

pra su stomam bharata vājayanta indrāya satyaṁ yadi satyam asti | nendro astīti nema u tva āha ka īṁ dadarśa kam abhi ṣṭavāma ||

**8.100.4**

ayam asmi jarataḥ paśya meha viśvā jātāny abhy asmi mahnā | ṛtasya mā pradiśo vardhayanty ādardiro bhuvanā dardarīmi ||

**8.100.5**

ā yan mā venā aruhaṇ ṛtasya ekam āsīnaṁ haryatasya pṛṣṭhe | manaś cin me hṛda ā praty avocad acikradañ chiśumantaḥ sakhāyaḥ ||

**8.100.6**

viśvet tā te savaneṣu pravācyā yā cakartha maghavann indra sunvate | pārāvataṁ yat purusambhṛtaṁ vasv apāvṛṇoḥ śarabhāya ṛṣibandhave ||

**8.100.7**

pra nūnaṁ dhāvatā pṛthaṅ neha yo vo avāvarīt | ni ṣīṁ vṛtrasya marmaṇi vajram indro apīpatat ||

**8.100.8**

manojavā ayamāna āyasīm atarat puram | divaṁ suparṇo gatvāya somaṁ vajriṇa ābharat ||

**8.100.9**

samudre antaḥ śayata udnā vajro abhīvṛtaḥ | bharanty asmai saṁyataḥ puraḥprasravaṇā balim ||

**8.100.10**

yad vāg vadanty avicetanāni rāṣṭrī devānāṁ niṣasāda mandrā | catasra ūrjaṁ duduhe payāṁsi kva svid asyāḥ paramaṁ jagāma ||

**8.100.11**

devīṁ vācam ajanayanta devās tāṁ viśvarūpāḥ paśavo vadanti | sā no mandreṣam ūrjaṁ duhānā dhenur vāg asmān upa suṣṭutaitu ||

**8.100.12**

sakhe viṣṇo vitaraṁ vi kramasva dyaur dehi lokaṁ vajrāya viṣkabhe | hanāva vṛtraṁ riṇacāva sindhūn indrasya yantu prasave visṛṣṭāḥ ||

**8.101.1**

ṛdhag itthā sa martyaḥ śaśame devatātaye | yo nūnam mitrāvaruṇāv abhiṣṭaya ācakre havyadātaye ||

**8.101.2**

varṣiṣṭhakṣatrā urucakṣasā narā rājānā dīrghaśruttamā | tā bāhutā na daṁsanā ratharyataḥ sākaṁ sūryasya raśmibhiḥ ||

**8.101.3**

pra yo vām mitrāvaruṇājiro dūto adravat | ayaḥśīrṣā maderaghuḥ ||

**8.101.4**

na yaḥ sampṛcche na punar havītave na saṁvādāya ramate | tasmān no adya samṛter uruṣyatam bāhubhyāṁ na uruṣyatam ||

**8.101.5**

pra mitrāya prāryamṇe sacathyam ṛtāvaso | varūthyaṁ varuṇe chandyaṁ vacaḥ stotraṁ rājasu gāyata ||

**8.101.6**

te hinvire aruṇaṁ jenyaṁ vasv ekam putraṁ tisṝṇām | te dhāmāny amṛtā martyānām adabdhā abhi cakṣate ||

**8.101.7**

ā me vacāṁsy udyatā dyumattamāni kartvā | ubhā yātaṁ nāsatyā sajoṣasā prati havyāni vītaye ||

**8.101.8**

rātiṁ yad vām arakṣasaṁ havāmahe yuvābhyāṁ vājinīvasū | prācīṁ hotrām pratirāntāv itaṁ narā gṛṇānā jamadagninā ||

**8.101.9**

ā no yajñaṁ divispṛśaṁ vāyo yāhi sumanmabhiḥ | antaḥ pavitra upari śrīṇāno 'yaṁ śukro ayāmi te ||

**8.101.10**

vety adhvaryuḥ pathibhī rajiṣṭhaiḥ prati havyāni vītaye | adhā niyutva ubhayasya naḥ piba śuciṁ somaṁ gavāśiram ||

**8.101.11**

baṇ mahām̐ asi sūrya baḻ āditya mahām̐ asi | mahas te sato mahimā panasyate 'ddhā deva mahām̐ asi ||

**8.101.12**

baṭ sūrya śravasā mahām̐ asi satrā deva mahām̐ asi | mahnā devānām asurya puroḥito vibhu jyotir adābhyam ||

**8.101.13**

iyaṁ yā nīcy arkiṇī rūpā rohiṇyā kṛtā | citreva praty adarśy āyaty antar daśasu bāhuṣu ||

**8.101.14**

prajā ha tisro atyāyam īyur ny anyā arkam abhito viviśre | bṛhad dha tasthau bhuvaneṣv antaḥ pavamāno harita ā viveśa ||

**8.101.15**

mātā rudrāṇāṁ duhitā vasūnāṁ svasāadityānām amṛtasya nābhiḥ | pra nu vocaṁ cikituṣe janāya mā gām anāgām aditiṁ vadhiṣṭa ||

**8.101.16**

vacovidaṁ vācam udīrayantīṁ viśvābhir dhībhir upatiṣṭhamānām | devīṁ devebhyaḥ pary eyuṣīṁ gām ā māvṛkta martyo dabhrācetāḥ ||

**8.102.1**

tvam agne bṛhad vayo dadhāsi deva dāśuṣe | kavir gṛhapatiryuvā ||

**8.102.2**

sa na īḻānayā saha devāmag̃ agne duvasyuvā | cikid vibhānav ā vaha ||

**8.102.3**

tvayā ha svid yujā vayaṁ codiṣṭhena yaviṣṭhya | abhi ṣmo vājasātaye ||

**8.102.4**

aurvabhṛguvac chucim apnavānavad ā huve | agniṁ samudravāsasam ||

**8.102.5**

huve vātasvanaṁ kavim parjanyakrandyaṁ sahaḥ | agniṁ samudravāsasam ||

**8.102.6**

ā savaṁ savitur yathā bhagasyeva bhujiṁ huve | agniṁ samudravāsasam ||

**8.102.7**

agniṁ vo vṛdhantam adhvarāṇām purūtamam | acchā naptre sahasvate ||

**8.102.8**

ayaṁ yathā na ābhuvat tvaṣṭā rūpeva takṣyā | asya kratvā yaśasvataḥ ||

**8.102.9**

ayaṁ viśvā abhi śriyo 'gnir deveṣu patyate | ā vājair upa no gamat ||

**8.102.10**

viśveṣām iha stuhi hotṝṇāṁ yaśastamam | agniṁ yajñeṣu pūrvyam ||

**8.102.11**

śīram pāvakaśociṣaṁ jyeṣṭho yo dameṣv ā | dīdāya dīrghaśruttamaḥ ||

**8.102.12**

tam arvantaṁ na sānasim gṛṇīhi vipra śuṣmiṇam | mitraṁ na yātayajjanam ||

**8.102.13**

upa tvā jāmayo giro dediśatīr haviṣkṛtaḥ | vāyor anīke asthiran ||

**8.102.14**

yasya tridhātv avṛtam barhis tasthāv asaṁdinam | āpaś cin ni dadhā padam ||

**8.102.15**

padaṁ devasya mīḻhuṣo 'nādhṛṣṭābhir ūtibhiḥ | bhadrā sūrya ivopa dṛk ||

**8.102.16**

agne ghṛtasya dhītibhis tepāno deva śociṣā | ā devān vakṣi yakṣi ca ||

**8.102.17**

taṁ tvājananta mātaraḥ kaviṁ devāso aṅgiraḥ | havyavāham amartyam ||

**8.102.18**

pracetasaṁ tvā kave 'gne dūtaṁ vareṇyam | havyavāhaṁ ni ṣedire ||

**8.102.19**

nahi me asty aghnyā na svadhitir vanavnati | athaitādṛg bhharāmi te ||

**8.102.20**

yad agne kāni kāni cid ā te dārūṇi dadhmasi | tā juṣasva yaviṣṭhya ||

**8.102.21**

yad atty upajihvikā yad vamro atisarpati | sarvaṁ tad astu te ghṛtam ||

**8.102.22**

agnim indhāno manasā dhiyaṁ saceta martyaḥ | agnim īdhe vivasvabhiḥ ||

**8.103.1**

adarśi gātuvittamo yasmin vratāny ādaduḥ | upo ṣu jātam āryasya vardhanam agniṁ nakṣanta no giraḥ ||

**8.103.2**

pra daivodāso agnir devāmg̃ acchā na majmanā | anu mātaram pṛthivīṁ vi vāvṛte tasthau nākasya sānavi ||

**8.103.3**

yasmād rejanta kṛṣṭayaś carkṛtyāni kṛṇvataḥ | sahasrasām medhasātāv iva tmanāgniṁ dhībhiḥ saparyata ||

**8.103.4**

pra yaṁ rāye ninīṣasi marto yas te vaso dāśat | sa vīraṁ dhatte agna ukthaśaṁsinaṁ tmanā sahasrapoṣiṇam ||

**8.103.5**

sa dṛḻhe cid abhi tṛṇatti vājam arvatā sa dhatte akṣiti śravaḥ | tve devatrā sadā purūvaso viśvā vāmāni dhīmahi ||

**8.103.6**

yo viśvā dayate vasu hotā mandro janānām | madhor na pātrā prathamāny asmai pra stomā yanty agnaye ||

**8.103.7**

aśvaṁ na gīrbhī rathyaṁ sudānavo marmṛjyante devayavaḥ | ubhe toke tanaye dasma viśpate parṣi rādho maghonām ||

**8.103.8**

pra maṁhiṣṭhāya gāyata ṛtāvne bṛhate śukraśociṣe | upastutāso agnaye ||

**8.103.9**

ā vaṁsate maghavā vīravad yaśaḥ samiddho dyu mny āhutaḥ | kuvin no asya sumatir navīyasy acchā vājebhir āgamat ||

**8.103.10**

preṣṭham u priyāṇāṁ stuhy āsāvātithim | agniṁ rathānāṁ yamam ||

**8.103.11**

uditā yo niditā veditā vasv ā yajñiyo vavartati | duṣṭarā yasya pravaṇe normayo dhiyā vājaṁ siṣāsataḥ ||

**8.103.12**

mā no hṛṇītām atithir vasur agniḥ pupraśasta eṣaḥ | yaḥ suhotā svadhvaraḥ ||

**8.103.13**

mo te riṣan ye achoktibhir vaso 'gne kebhiś cid evaiḥ | kīriś cid dhi tvām īṭṭe dūtyāya rātahavyaḥ svadhvaraḥ ||

**8.103.14**

āgne yāhi marutsakhā rudrebhiḥ somapītaye | sobharyā upa suṣṭutim mādayasva svarṇare ||
